1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    name: str,
   249→    description: str | None = None,
   250→    action: str | None = None,
   251→) -> Cluster:
   252→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   253→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   254→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   255→        raise ValueError(
   256→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   257→        )
   258→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   259→        raise ValueError(
   260→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for epics: {name!r}"
   261→        )
   262→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   263→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   264→    now = utc_now()
   265→    cluster: Cluster = {
   266→        "name": name,
   267→        "description": description,
   268→        "finding_ids": [],
   269→        "created_at": now,
   270→        "updated_at": now,
   271→        "auto": False,
   272→        "cluster_key": "",
   273→        "action": action,
   274→        "user_modified": False,
   275→    }
   276→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   277→    return cluster
   278→
   279→
   280→def add_to_cluster(
   281→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   282→) -> int:
   283→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   284→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   285→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   286→    if cluster is None:
   287→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   288→
   289→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   290→    count = 0
   291→    now = utc_now()
   292→    for fid in finding_ids:
   293→        if fid not in member_ids:
   294→            member_ids.append(fid)
   295→            count += 1
   296→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   297→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   298→        if fid not in overrides:
   299→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   300→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   301→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   302→
   303→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   304→    return count
   305→
   306→
   307→def remove_from_cluster(
   308→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   309→) -> int:
   310→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   311→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   312→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   313→    if cluster is None:
   314→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   315→
   316→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   317→    now = utc_now()
   318→    count = 0
   319→    for fid in finding_ids:
   320→        if fid in member_ids:
   321→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   322→            count += 1
   323→        # Clear cluster from override
   324→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   325→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   326→            override["cluster"] = None
   327→            override["updated_at"] = now
   328→
   329→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   330→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   331→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   332→
   333→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   334→    return count
   335→
   336→
   337→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   338→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   339→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   340→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   341→    if cluster is None:
   342→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   343→
   344→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   345→    now = utc_now()
   346→    for fid in orphaned:
   347→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   348→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   349→            override["cluster"] = None
   350→            override["updated_at"] = now
   351→
   352→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   353→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   354→
   355→    return orphaned
   356→
   357→
   358→def move_cluster(
   359→    plan: PlanModel,
   360→    cluster_name: str,
   361→    position: str,
   362→    target: str | None = None,
   363→    offset: int | None = None,
   364→) -> int:
   365→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   366→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   367→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   368→    if cluster is None:
   369→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   370→
   371→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   372→    if not member_ids:
   373→        return 0
   374→
   375→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   376→
   377→
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→# Focus
   380→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381→
   382→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   383→    """Set the active cluster focus.
   384→
   385→    Supports epic/ names — creates a transient cluster entry so the
   386→    queue filter can match epic finding_ids.
   387→    """
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    if cluster_name.startswith("epic/"):
   390→        epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   391→        if cluster_name not in epics:
   392→            raise ValueError(f"Epic {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   393→        epic = epics[cluster_name]
   394→        # Create transient cluster entry for queue filtering
   395→        plan["clusters"][cluster_name] = {
   396→            "name": cluster_name,
   397→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   398→            "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   399→            "auto": True,
   400→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{cluster_name}",
   401→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {cluster_name}",
   402→            "user_modified": False,
   403→        }
   404→        plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   405→        return
   406→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   407→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   408→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   409→
   410→
   411→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   412→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   413→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   414→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   415→
   416→
   417→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   418→# Reset
   419→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   420→
   421→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   422→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   423→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   424→    plan.clear()
   425→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   426→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   427→        plan[k] = v
   428→    plan["created"] = created
   429→
   430→
   431→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   432→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   433→
   434→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   435→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   436→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   437→    """
   438→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   439→    found = 0
   440→
   441→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   442→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   443→    for fid in finding_ids:
   444→        was_present = False
   445→        if fid in order:
   446→            order.remove(fid)
   447→            was_present = True
   448→        if fid in skipped:
   449→            skipped.pop(fid)
   450→            was_present = True
   451→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   452→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→            if fid in ids:
   454→                ids.remove(fid)
   455→                was_present = True
   456→        if was_present:
   457→            found += 1
   458→
   459→    return found
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "add_to_cluster",
   464→    "annotate_finding",
   465→    "clear_focus",
   466→    "create_cluster",
   467→    "delete_cluster",
   468→    "describe_finding",
   469→    "move_cluster",
   470→    "move_items",
   471→    "purge_ids",
   472→    "remove_from_cluster",
   473→    "reset_plan",
   474→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   475→    "set_focus",
   476→    "skip_items",
   477→    "unskip_items",
   478→]
   479→

    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}

    94→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    95→    version: Required[int]
    96→    created: Required[str]
    97→    updated: Required[str]
    98→    queue_order: list[str]
    99→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   100→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   101→    active_cluster: str | None
   102→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]

   108→
   109→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   110→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    return {
   113→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   114→        "created": now,
   115→        "updated": now,
   116→        "queue_order": [],
   117→        "deferred": [],
   118→        "skipped": {},
   119→        "active_cluster": None,
   120→        "overrides": {},
   121→        "clusters": {},
   122→        "superseded": {},
   123→        "epics": {},
   124→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   125→    }
   126→
   127→
   128→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   129→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   130→
   131→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
   132→    """
   133→    defaults = empty_plan()
   134→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   135→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   136→
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   138→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   140→        plan["deferred"] = []
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   142→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   144→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   146→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   148→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   149→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epics"), dict):
   150→        plan["epics"] = {}
   151→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   152→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   153→
   154→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   155→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   156→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   157→    if deferred:
   158→        now = utc_now()
   159→        for fid in list(deferred):
   160→            if fid not in skipped:
   161→                skipped[fid] = {
   162→                    "finding_id": fid,
   163→                    "kind": "temporary",
   164→                    "reason": None,
   165→                    "note": None,
   166→                    "attestation": None,
   167→                    "created_at": now,
   168→                    "review_after": None,
   169→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   170→                }
   171→        deferred.clear()
   172→
   173→    # Normalize cluster fields
   174→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   175→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   176→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   177→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   178→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   179→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   180→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   181→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   182→
   183→
   184→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   185→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   186→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   187→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   188→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   189→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   190→
   191→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   192→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   193→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   194→    if overlap:
   195→        raise ValueError(
   196→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   200→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   201→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   202→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   203→            raise ValueError(
   204→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   205→            )
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   210→    "Epic",
   211→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   212→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   213→    "Cluster",
   214→    "ItemOverride",
   215→    "PlanModel",
   216→    "SkipEntry",
   217→    "SupersededEntry",
   218→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   219→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   220→    "empty_plan",
   221→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   222→    "validate_plan",
   223→]
   224→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→    # Include epic finding_ids and dismissed lists
   147→    for epic in plan.get("epics", {}).values():
   148→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   149→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("dismissed", []))
   150→
   151→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   152→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   153→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   154→    referenced_ids.discard(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   155→    referenced_ids = {
   156→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   157→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   158→    }
   159→
   160→    # Check each referenced ID
   161→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   162→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   163→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   164→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   165→                result.changes += 1
   166→
   167→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   168→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   169→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   170→
   171→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   172→    if resurfaced:
   173→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   174→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   175→
   176→    # Prune old superseded entries
   177→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   178→    result.pruned = pruned
   179→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   180→
   181→    # Clean up epics: remove dead findings, mark completed, delete empty
   182→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   183→    dead_ids = {fid for fid in result.superseded}
   184→    for epic_name in list(epics.keys()):
   185→        epic = epics[epic_name]
   186→        fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   187→        alive = [fid for fid in fids if fid not in dead_ids]
   188→        if len(alive) != len(fids):
   189→            epic["finding_ids"] = alive
   190→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        # Mark completed if all findings resolved
   193→        if not alive and epic.get("status") != "completed":
   194→            epic["status"] = "completed"
   195→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   196→        # Delete empty epics
   197→        if not alive:
   198→            del epics[epic_name]
   199→
   200→    return result
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = [
   204→    "ReconcileResult",
   205→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   206→]
   207→

1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    11→    state: StateModel,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    15→    status_filter: str = "open",
    16→) -> list[str]:
    17→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    18→
    19→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    20→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    21→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    22→    """
    23→    seen: set[str] = set()
    24→    result: list[str] = []
    25→
    26→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    27→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    28→    if plan is not None:
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    30→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    31→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    32→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    33→
    34→    for pattern in patterns:
    35→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    36→        if matches:
    37→            for finding in matches:
    38→                fid = finding["id"]
    39→                if fid not in seen:
    40→                    seen.add(fid)
    41→                    result.append(fid)
    42→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    43→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    44→            seen.add(pattern)
    45→            result.append(pattern)
    46→    return result
    47→
    48→
    49→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    50→

    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None
    34→    status: str = "open"
    35→    include_subjective: bool = True
    36→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    37→    chronic: bool = False
    38→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    39→    explain: bool = False
    40→    plan: dict | None = None
    41→    include_skipped: bool = False

    44→
    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→) -> list[dict]:
    66→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    67→
    68→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    69→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    70→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    71→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    72→    """
    73→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    74→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    75→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    76→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    77→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    78→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    79→
    80→    # Build lookup
    81→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    84→
    85→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    86→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    87→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    88→        if override.get("description"):
    89→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    90→        if override.get("note"):
    91→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    92→        if override.get("cluster"):
    93→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    94→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    95→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    96→                "name": cluster_name,
    97→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    98→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    99→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   100→            }
   101→
   102→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   103→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   104→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for fid in queue_order:
   106→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   107→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   108→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   109→
   110→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   111→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   112→    for item in items:
   113→        item_id = item["id"]
   114→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   115→            continue
   116→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   117→            skipped_items.append(item)
   118→        else:
   119→            remaining.append(item)
   120→
   121→    # Assign queue positions
   122→    result = ordered + remaining
   123→    if include_skipped:
   124→        result = result + skipped_items
   125→
   126→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   127→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   128→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   129→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   130→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   131→            if skip_entry:
   132→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   133→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   134→                if skip_reason:
   135→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   136→
   137→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   138→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   139→    if effective_cluster:
   140→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   141→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   142→        if cluster_member_ids:
   143→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   144→
   145→    return result
   146→
   147→
   148→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   149→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   150→    kind = item.get("kind", "")

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 3
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
    14→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    15→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→
    20→
    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→
    31→
    32→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    33→    finding_id: Required[str]
    34→    description: str | None
    35→    note: str | None
    36→    cluster: str | None
    37→    created_at: str
    38→    updated_at: str
    39→
    40→
    41→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    42→    name: Required[str]
    43→    description: str | None
    44→    finding_ids: list[str]
    45→    created_at: str
    46→    updated_at: str
    47→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    48→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    49→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    50→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    51→
    52→
    53→class Epic(TypedDict, total=False):
    54→    name: Required[str]
    55→    thesis: Required[str]
    56→    direction: Required[str]  # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
    57→    root_cause: str
    58→    finding_ids: list[str]
    59→    supersedes: list[str]
    60→    dismissed: list[str]
    61→    agent_safe: bool
    62→    dependency_order: int
    63→    action_steps: list[str]
    64→    source_clusters: list[str]
    65→    status: str  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
    66→    created_at: str
    67→    updated_at: str
    68→    synthesis_version: int
    69→    auto: bool  # Always True (LLM-generated)
    70→
    71→
    72→class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    73→    synthesized_ids: list[str]
    74→    last_run: str
    75→    version: int
    76→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    77→    finding_snapshot_hash: str
    78→    strategy_summary: str
    79→    trigger: str
    80→
    81→
    82→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    83→    original_id: Required[str]
    84→    original_detector: str
    85→    original_file: str
    86→    original_summary: str
    87→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    88→    superseded_at: str
    89→    remapped_to: str | None
    90→    candidates: list[str]
    91→    note: str | None
    92→
    93→
    94→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    95→    version: Required[int]
    96→    created: Required[str]
    97→    updated: Required[str]
    98→    queue_order: list[str]
    99→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   100→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   101→    active_cluster: str | None
   102→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   103→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   104→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   105→    epics: dict[str, Epic]
   106→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   107→
   108→
   109→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   110→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    return {
   113→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   114→        "created": now,
   115→        "updated": now,
   116→        "queue_order": [],
   117→        "deferred": [],
   118→        "skipped": {},
   119→        "active_cluster": None,
   120→        "overrides": {},
   121→        "clusters": {},
   122→        "superseded": {},
   123→        "epics": {},
   124→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   125→    }
   126→
   127→
   128→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   129→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   130→
   131→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
   132→    """
   133→    defaults = empty_plan()
   134→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   135→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   136→
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   138→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   140→        plan["deferred"] = []
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   142→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   144→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   146→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   148→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   149→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epics"), dict):
   150→        plan["epics"] = {}
   151→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   152→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   153→
   154→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   155→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   156→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   157→    if deferred:
   158→        now = utc_now()
   159→        for fid in list(deferred):
   160→            if fid not in skipped:
   161→                skipped[fid] = {
   162→                    "finding_id": fid,
   163→                    "kind": "temporary",
   164→                    "reason": None,
   165→                    "note": None,
   166→                    "attestation": None,
   167→                    "created_at": now,
   168→                    "review_after": None,
   169→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   170→                }
   171→        deferred.clear()
   172→
   173→    # Normalize cluster fields
   174→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   175→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   176→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   177→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   178→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   179→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   180→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   181→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   182→
   183→
   184→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   185→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   186→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   187→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   188→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   189→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   190→
   191→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   192→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   193→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   194→    if overlap:
   195→        raise ValueError(
   196→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   200→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   201→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   202→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   203→            raise ValueError(
   204→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   205→            )
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   210→    "Epic",
   211→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   212→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   213→    "Cluster",
   214→    "ItemOverride",
   215→    "PlanModel",
   216→    "SkipEntry",
   217→    "SupersededEntry",
   218→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   219→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   220→    "empty_plan",
   221→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   222→    "validate_plan",
   223→]
   224→

    27→class QueueBuildOptions:

    31→    count: int | None = 1

    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]

    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→) -> list[dict]:
    66→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    67→
    68→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    69→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    70→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    71→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    72→    """
    73→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    74→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    75→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    76→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    77→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    78→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    79→
    80→    # Build lookup
    81→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    84→
    85→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    86→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    87→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    88→        if override.get("description"):
    89→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    90→        if override.get("note"):
    91→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    92→        if override.get("cluster"):
    93→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    94→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    95→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    96→                "name": cluster_name,
    97→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    98→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    99→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   100→            }
   101→
   102→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   103→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   104→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for fid in queue_order:
   106→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   107→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   108→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   109→
   110→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   111→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   112→    for item in items:
   113→        item_id = item["id"]
   114→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   115→            continue
   116→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   117→            skipped_items.append(item)
   118→        else:
   119→            remaining.append(item)
   120→
   121→    # Assign queue positions
   122→    result = ordered + remaining
   123→    if include_skipped:
   124→        result = result + skipped_items
   125→
   126→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   127→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   128→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   129→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   130→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   131→            if skip_entry:
   132→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   133→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   134→                if skip_reason:
   135→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   136→
   137→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   138→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   139→    if effective_cluster:
   140→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   141→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   142→        if cluster_member_ids:
   143→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   144→
   145→    return result
   146→
   147→
   148→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   149→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   150→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   151→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   152→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   153→    if kind == "cluster":
   154→        return any(
   155→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   156→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   157→        )
   158→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   159→
   160→
   161→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   162→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   163→    try:
   164→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   165→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   166→        if meta:
   167→            return meta.action_type
   168→    except ImportError:
   169→        pass
   170→    return "manual_fix"
   171→
   172→
   173→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   174→
   175→
   176→def _epic_membership(epics: dict) -> dict[str, str]:
   177→    """Build mapping of finding id to epic name."""
   178→    finding_to_epic: dict[str, str] = {}
   179→    for epic_name, epic in epics.items():
   180→        if epic.get("status") == "completed":
   181→            continue
   182→        for finding_id in epic.get("finding_ids", []):
   183→            finding_to_epic[finding_id] = epic_name
   184→    return finding_to_epic
   185→
   186→
   187→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   188→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress epic."""
   189→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   190→    if not epics:
   191→        return []
   192→
   193→    total_epics = len(epics)
   194→    items: list[dict] = []
   195→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   196→        epics.items(),
   197→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   198→    ):
   199→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   200→        if status == "completed":
   201→            continue
   202→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→        if not finding_ids:
   204→            continue
   205→
   206→        items.append({
   207→            "id": epic_name,
   208→            "kind": "epic",
   209→            "tier": 1,
   210→            "effective_tier": 1,
   211→            "confidence": "high",
   212→            "detector": "epic",
   213→            "file": ".",
   214→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   215→            "status": "open",
   216→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   217→            "detail": {
   218→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   219→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   220→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   221→                "epic_status": status,
   222→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   223→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   224→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   225→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   226→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   227→            },
   228→        })
   229→    return items
   230→
   231→
   232→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   233→    *,
   234→    all_items: list[dict],
   235→    plan: dict,
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    scope: str | None,
   238→) -> None:
   239→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   240→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   241→
   242→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   243→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   244→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   245→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   246→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   247→
   248→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   249→    for item in epic_items:
   250→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   251→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   252→                all_items.append(item)
   253→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   254→
   255→
   256→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   257→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   258→
   259→    Epic membership takes precedence: findings in epics are excluded
   260→    from cluster collapsing (they'll be represented by epic items).
   261→    """
   262→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   263→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   264→
   265→    # Build epic membership to exclude from cluster collapse
   266→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   267→    if epics:
   268→        for epic in epics.values():
   269→            if epic.get("status") != "completed":
   270→                epic_finding_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   271→
   272→    if not clusters:
   273→        return items
   274→
   275→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   276→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   277→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   278→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   279→            continue
   280→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   281→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   282→
   283→    # Remove epic findings from cluster membership
   284→    for fid in epic_finding_ids:
   285→        fid_to_cluster.pop(fid, None)
   286→
   287→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   288→        return items
   289→
   290→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   291→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   292→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   293→
   294→    for item in items:
   295→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   296→        if cname:
   297→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   298→        else:
   299→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   300→
   301→    # Build cluster meta-items
   302→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   303→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   304→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   305→        if len(members) < 2:
   306→            result.extend(members)
   307→            continue
   308→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   309→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   310→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   311→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   312→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   313→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   314→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   315→            action_type = "reorganize"
   316→        else:
   317→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   318→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   319→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   320→                action_type = "refactor"
   321→
   322→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   323→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   324→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   325→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   326→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   327→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   328→        else:
   329→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   330→
   331→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   332→        if not primary_command:
   333→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   334→
   335→        meta_item: dict = {
   336→            "id": cname,
   337→            "kind": "cluster",
   338→            "action_type": action_type,
   339→            "summary": summary,
   340→            "members": members,
   341→            "member_count": len(members),
   342→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   343→            "cluster_name": cname,
   344→            "cluster_auto": True,
   345→            "confidence": "high",
   346→            "detector": detector,
   347→            "file": "",
   348→        }
   349→        result.append(meta_item)
   350→
   351→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   352→    return result
   353→
   354→
   355→def build_work_queue(
   356→    state: StateModel,
   357→    *,
   358→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   359→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   360→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   361→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   362→
   363→    status = resolved_options.status
   364→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   365→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   366→    try:
   367→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   368→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   369→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   370→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   371→
   372→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   373→        state,
   374→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   375→        status_filter=status,
   376→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   377→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   381→    if (
   382→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   383→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   384→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   385→    ):
   386→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   387→            state,
   388→            state.get("findings", {}),
   389→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   390→        )
   391→        for item in subjective_items:
   392→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   393→                all_items.append(item)
   394→
   395→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   396→    if resolved_options.plan:
   397→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   398→            all_items=all_items,
   399→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   400→            state=state,
   401→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   402→        )
   403→
   404→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   405→
   406→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   407→    if resolved_options.plan:
   408→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   409→            all_items,
   410→            resolved_options.plan,
   411→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   412→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   413→        )
   414→
   415→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   416→    should_collapse = (
   417→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   418→        and resolved_options.plan
   419→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   420→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   421→    )
   422→    if should_collapse:
   423→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   424→
   425→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   426→
   427→    requested_tier = (
   428→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   429→    )
   430→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   431→    fallback_reason = None
   432→    filtered = all_items
   433→
   434→    if requested_tier is not None:
   435→        filtered = [
   436→            item
   437→            for item in all_items
   438→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   439→        ]
   440→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   441→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   442→            if chosen is not None:
   443→                selected_tier = chosen
   444→                filtered = [
   445→                    item
   446→                    for item in all_items
   447→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   448→                ]
   449→                fallback_reason = (
   450→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   451→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   452→                )
   453→        elif not filtered:
   454→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   455→
   456→    total = len(filtered)
   457→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   458→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   459→
   460→    if resolved_options.explain:
   461→        for item in filtered:
   462→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   463→
   464→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   465→    return {
   466→        "items": filtered,
   467→        "total": total,
   468→        "tier_counts": counts,
   469→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   470→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   471→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   472→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   473→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   474→    }
   475→
   476→
   477→__all__ = [
   478→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   479→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   480→    "WorkQueueResult",
   481→    "build_work_queue",
   482→    "group_queue_items",
   483→]
   484→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→    # Include epic finding_ids and dismissed lists
   147→    for epic in plan.get("epics", {}).values():
   148→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   149→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("dismissed", []))
   150→
   151→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   152→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   153→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   154→    referenced_ids.discard(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   155→    referenced_ids = {
   156→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   157→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   158→    }
   159→
   160→    # Check each referenced ID
   161→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   162→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   163→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   164→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   165→                result.changes += 1
   166→
   167→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   168→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   169→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   170→
   171→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   172→    if resurfaced:
   173→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   174→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   175→
   176→    # Prune old superseded entries
   177→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   178→    result.pruned = pruned
   179→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   180→
   181→    # Clean up epics: remove dead findings, mark completed, delete empty
   182→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   183→    dead_ids = {fid for fid in result.superseded}
   184→    for epic_name in list(epics.keys()):
   185→        epic = epics[epic_name]
   186→        fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   187→        alive = [fid for fid in fids if fid not in dead_ids]
   188→        if len(alive) != len(fids):
   189→            epic["finding_ids"] = alive
   190→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        # Mark completed if all findings resolved
   193→        if not alive and epic.get("status") != "completed":
   194→            epic["status"] = "completed"
   195→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   196→        # Delete empty epics
   197→        if not alive:
   198→            del epics[epic_name]
   199→
   200→    return result
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = [
   204→    "ReconcileResult",
   205→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   206→]
   207→

   246→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   247→

   256→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   257→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   258→
   259→    Epic membership takes precedence: findings in epics are excluded
   260→    from cluster collapsing (they'll be represented by epic items).
   261→    """
   262→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   263→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   264→
   265→    # Build epic membership to exclude from cluster collapse
   266→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   267→    if epics:
   268→        for epic in epics.values():
   269→            if epic.get("status") != "completed":
   270→                epic_finding_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   271→
   272→    if not clusters:
   273→        return items
   274→
   275→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   276→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   277→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   278→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   279→            continue
   280→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   281→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   282→
   283→    # Remove epic findings from cluster membership
   284→    for fid in epic_finding_ids:
   285→        fid_to_cluster.pop(fid, None)
   286→
   287→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   288→        return items
   289→
   290→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   291→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   292→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   293→
   294→    for item in items:
   295→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   296→        if cname:
   297→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   298→        else:
   299→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   300→
   301→    # Build cluster meta-items
   302→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   303→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   304→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   305→        if len(members) < 2:
   306→            result.extend(members)
   307→            continue
   308→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   309→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   310→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   311→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   312→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   313→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   314→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   315→            action_type = "reorganize"
   316→        else:
   317→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   318→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   319→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   320→                action_type = "refactor"
   321→
   322→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   323→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   324→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   325→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   326→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   327→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   328→        else:
   329→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   330→
   331→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   332→        if not primary_command:
   333→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   334→
   335→        meta_item: dict = {
   336→            "id": cname,
   337→            "kind": "cluster",
   338→            "action_type": action_type,
   339→            "summary": summary,
   340→            "members": members,
   341→            "member_count": len(members),
   342→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   343→            "cluster_name": cname,
   344→            "cluster_auto": True,
   345→            "confidence": "high",
   346→            "detector": detector,
   347→            "file": "",
   348→        }
   349→        result.append(meta_item)
   350→
   351→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   352→    return result
