    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))

   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→
   371→
   372→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   373→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   374→    if not plan:
   375→        return
   376→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   377→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   378→    if not summary:
   379→        return
   380→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   381→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   382→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   383→
   384→
   385→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   386→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   387→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   389→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   390→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   391→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   392→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   393→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   394→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   395→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   396→
   397→    print(colorize("  (Meta-plan update needed, T1)", "bold"))
   398→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   399→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   400→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   401→    print()
   402→    if new:
   403→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   404→    if resolved:
   405→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   406→    if epic_count:
   407→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   408→    print()
   409→    print("  Action:   desloppify plan synthesize")
   410→    print("  This will show findings and guide you through creating clusters,")
   411→    print("  setting priorities, and organizing the meta-plan.")
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   414→    print()
   415→    print("  View plan:")
   416→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   417→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   418→
   419→
   420→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   421→    """Render an epic card."""
   422→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   423→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   424→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   425→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   426→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   427→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   428→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   429→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   430→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   431→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   432→
   433→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   434→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   435→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   436→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   437→    if root_cause:
   438→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   439→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   440→    if total_epics:
   441→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   442→    print()
   443→    if action_steps:
   444→        print("  Steps:")
   445→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   446→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   447→        print()
   448→    if finding_ids:
   449→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   450→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   451→        for fid in shown:
   452→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   453→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   454→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   455→        print()
   456→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   457→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   458→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   459→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   460→
   461→
   462→def render_terminal_items(
   463→    items: list[dict],
   464→    dim_scores: dict,
   465→    findings_scoped: dict,
   466→    *,
   467→    group: str,
   468→    explain: bool,
   469→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   470→    plan: dict | None = None,
   471→) -> None:
   472→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   473→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   474→
   475→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   476→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   477→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   478→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   479→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   480→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   481→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   482→
   483→        # Nudge about pending synthesis hidden by focus
   484→        from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   485→        if SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   486→            if not any(it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed" for it in items):
   487→                print(colorize(
   488→                    "  Note: meta-plan synthesis is pending -- run "
   489→                    "`desloppify plan synthesize` when ready.",
   490→                    "yellow",
   491→                ))
   492→
   493→    if group != "item":
   494→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   495→        return
   496→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   497→        if idx > 0:
   498→            print()
   499→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   500→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   501→
   502→        # Special rendering for synthesis and epic items
   503→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   504→            _render_synthesis_item(item)
   505→            continue
   506→        if kind == "epic":
   507→            _render_epic_item(item)
   508→            continue
   509→
   510→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   511→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   512→        elif len(items) > 1:
   513→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   514→        else:
   515→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   516→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   517→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   518→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   519→
   520→
   521→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   522→    if len(items) != 1:
   523→        return
   524→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   525→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   526→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   527→    if kind != "finding":
   528→        return
   529→    item = items[0]
   530→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   531→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   532→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   533→        primary = item.get(
   534→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   535→        )
   536→        print(f"    {primary}")
   537→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   538→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   539→        return
   540→
   541→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   542→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   543→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   544→        print(f"    {primary}")
   545→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   546→    else:
   547→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   548→
   549→    print(
   550→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   551→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   552→    )
   553→    print(
   554→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   555→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   556→    )
   557→
   558→
   559→def render_followup_nudges(
   560→    state: dict,
   561→    dim_scores: dict,
   562→    findings_scoped: dict,
   563→    *,
   564→    strict_score: float | None,
   565→    target_strict_score: float,
   566→) -> None:
   567→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   568→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   569→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   570→        state,
   571→        subjective_entries,
   572→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   573→        max_quality_items=3,
   574→        max_integrity_items=5,
   575→    )
   576→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   577→    if strict_score is not None:
   578→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   579→        if gap > 0:
   580→            print(
   581→                colorize(
   582→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   583→                    "cyan",
   584→                )
   585→            )
   586→        else:
   587→            print(
   588→                colorize(
   589→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   590→                    "green",
   591→                )
   592→            )
   593→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   594→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   595→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   596→
   597→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   598→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   599→        findings_scoped
   600→    )
   601→    parts: list[str] = []
   602→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   603→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   604→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   605→    open_review = [
   606→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   607→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   608→    ]
   609→    if low_dims:
   610→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   611→    if stale_count:
   612→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   613→    if unassessed_count:
   614→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   615→    if len(open_review):
   616→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   617→    if coverage_open > 0:
   618→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   619→
   620→    if parts:
   621→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   622→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   623→
   624→
   625→__all__ = [
   626→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   627→    "render_followup_nudges",
   628→    "render_queue_header",
   629→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   630→    "render_terminal_items",
   631→    "scorecard_subjective",
   632→    "show_empty_queue",
   633→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   634→]
   635→

1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    name: str,
   249→    description: str | None = None,
   250→    action: str | None = None,
   251→) -> Cluster:
   252→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   253→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   254→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   255→        raise ValueError(
   256→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   257→        )
   258→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   259→        raise ValueError(
   260→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for epics: {name!r}"
   261→        )
   262→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   263→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   264→    now = utc_now()
   265→    cluster: Cluster = {
   266→        "name": name,
   267→        "description": description,
   268→        "finding_ids": [],
   269→        "created_at": now,
   270→        "updated_at": now,
   271→        "auto": False,
   272→        "cluster_key": "",
   273→        "action": action,
   274→        "user_modified": False,
   275→    }
   276→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   277→    return cluster
   278→
   279→
   280→def add_to_cluster(
   281→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   282→) -> int:
   283→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   284→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   285→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   286→    if cluster is None:
   287→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   288→
   289→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   290→    count = 0
   291→    now = utc_now()
   292→    for fid in finding_ids:
   293→        if fid not in member_ids:
   294→            member_ids.append(fid)
   295→            count += 1
   296→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   297→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   298→        if fid not in overrides:
   299→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   300→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   301→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   302→
   303→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   304→    return count
   305→
   306→
   307→def remove_from_cluster(
   308→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   309→) -> int:
   310→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   311→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   312→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   313→    if cluster is None:
   314→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   315→
   316→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   317→    now = utc_now()
   318→    count = 0
   319→    for fid in finding_ids:
   320→        if fid in member_ids:
   321→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   322→            count += 1
   323→        # Clear cluster from override
   324→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   325→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   326→            override["cluster"] = None
   327→            override["updated_at"] = now
   328→
   329→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   330→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   331→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   332→
   333→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   334→    return count
   335→
   336→
   337→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   338→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   339→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   340→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   341→    if cluster is None:
   342→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   343→
   344→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   345→    now = utc_now()
   346→    for fid in orphaned:
   347→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   348→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   349→            override["cluster"] = None
   350→            override["updated_at"] = now
   351→
   352→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   353→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   354→
   355→    return orphaned
   356→
   357→
   358→def move_cluster(
   359→    plan: PlanModel,
   360→    cluster_name: str,
   361→    position: str,
   362→    target: str | None = None,
   363→    offset: int | None = None,
   364→) -> int:
   365→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   366→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   367→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   368→    if cluster is None:
   369→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   370→
   371→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   372→    if not member_ids:
   373→        return 0
   374→
   375→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   376→
   377→
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→# Focus
   380→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381→
   382→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   383→    """Set the active cluster focus.
   384→
   385→    Supports epic/ names — creates a transient cluster entry so the
   386→    queue filter can match epic finding_ids.
   387→    """
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    if cluster_name.startswith("epic/"):
   390→        epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   391→        if cluster_name not in epics:
   392→            raise ValueError(f"Epic {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   393→        epic = epics[cluster_name]
   394→        # Create transient cluster entry for queue filtering
   395→        plan["clusters"][cluster_name] = {
   396→            "name": cluster_name,
   397→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   398→            "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   399→            "auto": True,
   400→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{cluster_name}",
   401→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {cluster_name}",
   402→            "user_modified": False,
   403→        }
   404→        plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   405→        return
   406→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   407→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   408→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   409→
   410→
   411→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   412→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   413→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   414→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   415→
   416→
   417→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   418→# Reset
   419→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   420→
   421→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   422→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   423→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   424→    plan.clear()
   425→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   426→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   427→        plan[k] = v
   428→    plan["created"] = created
   429→
   430→
   431→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   432→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   433→
   434→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   435→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   436→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   437→    """
   438→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   439→    found = 0
   440→
   441→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   442→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   443→    for fid in finding_ids:
   444→        was_present = False
   445→        if fid in order:
   446→            order.remove(fid)
   447→            was_present = True
   448→        if fid in skipped:
   449→            skipped.pop(fid)
   450→            was_present = True
   451→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   452→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→            if fid in ids:
   454→                ids.remove(fid)
   455→                was_present = True
   456→        if was_present:
   457→            found += 1
   458→
   459→    return found
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "add_to_cluster",
   464→    "annotate_finding",
   465→    "clear_focus",
   466→    "create_cluster",
   467→    "delete_cluster",
   468→    "describe_finding",
   469→    "move_cluster",
   470→    "move_items",
   471→    "purge_ids",
   472→    "remove_from_cluster",
   473→    "reset_plan",
   474→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   475→    "set_focus",
   476→    "skip_items",
   477→    "unskip_items",
   478→]
   479→

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   101→    # detector::security → auto/security
   102→    parts = key.split("::")
   103→    if len(parts) == 2:
   104→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   105→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   106→        if prefix_type == "review":
   107→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   108→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   109→    if len(parts) == 3:
   110→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   111→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Description and action generation
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→def _generate_description(
   119→    cluster_name: str,
   120→    members: list[dict],
   121→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   122→    subtype: str | None,
   123→) -> str:
   124→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   125→    count = len(members)
   126→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   127→
   128→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   129→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   130→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   131→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   132→
   133→    if detector == "structural":
   134→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   135→        if len(files) == 1:
   136→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   137→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   138→
   139→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   140→    if subtype:
   141→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   142→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   143→
   144→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   145→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   146→
   147→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   148→
   149→
   150→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   151→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   152→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   153→        return None
   154→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   155→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   156→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   157→        return fixer_name
   158→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   159→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   160→        if subtype in fixer:
   161→            return fixer
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   166→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   167→
   168→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   169→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   170→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   171→    """
   172→    if " — " in guidance:
   173→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   174→    return guidance
   175→
   176→
   177→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   178→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   179→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   180→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   181→}
   182→
   183→
   184→def _generate_action(
   185→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   186→    subtype: str | None,
   187→) -> str:
   188→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   189→
   190→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   191→    """
   192→    if meta is None:
   193→        return "review and fix each finding"
   194→
   195→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   196→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   197→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   198→        if matched_fixer:
   199→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   200→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   201→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   202→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   203→
   204→    if meta.tool == "move":
   205→        return "desloppify move"
   206→
   207→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   208→    if meta.guidance:
   209→        if subtype:
   210→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   211→        return meta.guidance
   212→
   213→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   214→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   215→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   220→# Main algorithm
   221→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   222→
   223→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   224→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   225→
   226→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   227→    """
   228→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   229→    changes = 0
   230→
   231→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   232→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   233→
   234→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   235→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   236→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   237→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   238→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   239→
   240→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   241→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   242→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   243→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   244→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   245→            continue
   246→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   247→            continue
   248→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   252→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   253→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   254→        if key is None:
   255→            continue
   256→
   257→        groups[key].append(fid)
   258→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   259→
   260→    # Drop singleton groups
   261→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   262→
   263→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   264→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   265→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   266→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   267→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   268→            if ck:
   269→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   270→
   271→    now = utc_now()
   272→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   273→
   274→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   275→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   276→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   277→
   278→        # Representative finding for metadata
   279→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   280→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   281→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   282→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   283→
   284→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   285→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   286→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   287→
   288→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   289→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   290→
   291→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   292→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   293→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   294→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   295→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   296→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   297→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   298→                if new_ids:
   299→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   300→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   301→                    changes += 1
   302→            else:
   303→                # Replace membership wholesale
   304→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   305→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   306→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   307→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   308→                    cluster["description"] = description
   309→                    cluster["action"] = action
   310→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   311→                    changes += 1
   312→        else:
   313→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   314→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   315→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   316→                # Append a disambiguator
   317→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   318→
   319→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   320→                "name": cluster_name,
   321→                "description": description,
   322→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   323→                "created_at": now,
   324→                "updated_at": now,
   325→                "auto": True,
   326→                "cluster_key": key,
   327→                "action": action,
   328→                "user_modified": False,
   329→            }
   330→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   331→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   332→            changes += 1
   333→
   334→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   335→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   336→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   337→        for fid in member_ids:
   338→            if fid not in overrides:
   339→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   340→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   341→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   342→
   343→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   344→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   345→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   346→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   347→    )
   348→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   349→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   350→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   351→    )
   352→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   353→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   357→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   358→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   359→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   360→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   361→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   362→
   363→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   364→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   365→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   366→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   367→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   368→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   369→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   370→                cluster["description"] = description
   371→                cluster["action"] = action
   372→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   373→                changes += 1
   374→        else:
   375→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   376→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   377→                "description": description,
   378→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   379→                "created_at": now,
   380→                "updated_at": now,
   381→                "auto": True,
   382→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   383→                "action": action,
   384→                "user_modified": False,
   385→            }
   386→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   387→            changes += 1
   388→
   389→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   390→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   391→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   392→            if fid not in overrides:
   393→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   394→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   395→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   396→
   397→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   398→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   399→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   400→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   401→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   402→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   403→
   404→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   405→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   406→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   407→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   408→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   409→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   410→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   411→                cluster["description"] = description
   412→                cluster["action"] = action
   413→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   414→                changes += 1
   415→        else:
   416→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   417→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   418→                "description": description,
   419→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   420→                "created_at": now,
   421→                "updated_at": now,
   422→                "auto": True,
   423→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   424→                "action": action,
   425→                "user_modified": False,
   426→            }
   427→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   428→            changes += 1
   429→
   430→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   431→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   432→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   433→            if fid not in overrides:
   434→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   435→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   436→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   437→
   438→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   439→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   440→        cluster = clusters[name]
   441→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   442→            continue
   443→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   444→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   445→            continue
   446→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   447→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   448→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   449→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   450→            if alive:
   451→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   452→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   453→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   454→                    changes += 1
   455→                continue
   456→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   457→        # Delete stale cluster
   458→        del clusters[name]
   459→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   460→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   461→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   462→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   463→                override["cluster"] = None
   464→                override["updated_at"] = now
   465→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   466→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   467→        changes += 1
   468→
   469→    plan["updated"] = now
   470→    return changes
   471→
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   475→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   476→]
   477→

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )

1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause into epics, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing epics rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    13→from typing import Any
    14→
    15→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    16→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    17→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    18→    Epic,
    19→    PlanModel,
    20→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    23→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    24→
    25→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    26→
    27→
    28→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    29→# Data types
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→
    32→@dataclass
    33→class SynthesisInput:
    34→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
    35→
    36→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
    37→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
    38→    existing_epics: dict[str, Epic]       # current epics to update
    39→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
    40→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last synthesis
    41→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
    42→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior synthesis
    43→    synthesis_version: int               # next version number
    44→
    45→
    46→@dataclass
    47→class DismissedFinding:
    48→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
    49→
    50→    finding_id: str
    51→    reason: str
    52→
    53→
    54→@dataclass
    55→class ContradictionNote:
    56→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
    57→
    58→    kept: str
    59→    dismissed: str
    60→    reason: str
    61→
    62→
    63→@dataclass
    64→class SynthesisResult:
    65→    """Parsed and validated LLM synthesis output."""
    66→
    67→    strategy_summary: str
    68→    epics: list[dict]
    69→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
    70→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
    71→    priority_rationale: str = ""
    72→
    73→
    74→@dataclass
    75→class SynthesisMutationResult:
    76→    """What changed when synthesis was applied to the plan."""
    77→
    78→    epics_created: int = 0
    79→    epics_updated: int = 0
    80→    epics_completed: int = 0
    81→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
    82→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
    83→    strategy_summary: str = ""
    84→    synthesis_version: int = 0
    85→    dry_run: bool = False
    86→
    87→
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→# Input collection
    90→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    91→
    92→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
    93→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
    94→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    95→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    96→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    97→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    98→
    99→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   100→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   101→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   102→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   103→            continue
   104→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   105→            open_review[fid] = f
   106→        else:
   107→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   108→
   109→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   110→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   111→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   112→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   113→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   114→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   115→
   116→    return SynthesisInput(
   117→        open_findings=open_review,
   118→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   119→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   120→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   121→        new_since_last=new_since,
   122→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   123→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   124→        synthesis_version=version,
   125→    )
   126→
   127→
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→# Prompt construction
   130→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   131→
   132→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   133→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   134→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   135→
   136→Your plan should:
   137→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   138→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   139→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   140→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   141→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   142→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   143→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   144→
   145→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   146→
   147→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   148→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   149→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   150→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
```
