```
100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   169→
   170→
   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:
   174→        return {}
   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:
   179→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   180→        if not name:
   181→            continue
   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   297→    """Build a synthetic T1 queue item for pending epic synthesis.
   298→
   299→    Returns None if synthesis is not needed (no synthesis::pending in queue).
   300→    """
   301→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   302→        SYNTHESIS_ID,
   303→        review_finding_snapshot_hash,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   307→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
   308→        return None
   309→
   310→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   311→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   312→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   313→
   314→    # Count review findings
   315→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   316→    review_ids = [
   317→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   318→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   319→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   320→    ]
   321→    total = len(review_ids)
   322→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   323→    new_since_last = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid not in synthesized)
   324→    resolved_since_last = sum(1 for fid in synthesized if fid not in {r for r in review_ids})
   325→
   326→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   327→    pending_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status", "pending") == "pending")
   328→    in_progress_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "in_progress")
   329→    completed_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "completed")
   330→
   331→    trigger = "initial synthesis needed"
   332→    if last_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   333→        if new_since_last > resolved_since_last:
   334→            trigger = "new findings imported"
   335→        elif resolved_since_last > 0:
   336→            trigger = "findings resolved"
   337→        else:
   338→            trigger = "findings changed"
   339→
   340→    return {
   341→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   342→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   343→        "tier": 1,
   344→        "effective_tier": 1,
   345→        "confidence": "high",
   346→        "detector": "synthesis",
   347→        "file": ".",
   348→        "summary": f"Meta-plan update: {total} review findings need integration into epics",
   349→        "status": "open",
   350→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   351→        "detail": {
   352→            "total_review_findings": total,
   353→            "new_since_last": new_since_last,
   354→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since_last,
   355→            "trigger": trigger,
   356→            "epic_count": len(epics),
   357→            "pending_epics": pending_epics,
   358→            "in_progress_epics": in_progress_epics,
   359→            "completed_epics": completed_epics,
   360→        },
   361→    }
   362→
   363→
   364→__all__ = [
   365→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   366→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   367→    "build_subjective_items",
   368→    "build_synthesis_item",
   369→    "detail_dict",
   370→    "is_review_finding",
   371→    "is_subjective_finding",
   372→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   373→    "review_finding_weight",
   374→    "scope_matches",
   375→    "slugify",
   376→    "status_matches",
   377→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   378→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   379→]
   380→
```

```
     2→

     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (

    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    88→
    89→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before everything else (T0-equivalent).
    90→    if kind == "synthesis_needed":
    91→        return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    92→    if kind == "epic":
    93→        dep_order = 999
    94→        detail = item.get("detail")
    95→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    96→            dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
    97→        return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    98→
    99→    if kind == "cluster":
   100→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
```

```
   133→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   134→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   135→
   136→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":

   144→            ),
   145→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   146→        }

   149→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   150→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   151→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   152→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   153→    if is_subjective:
   154→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   155→    elif is_review:
   156→        ranking_factors = [
   157→            "tier asc",
   158→            "confidence asc",
   159→            "review_weight desc",
   160→            "count desc",
   161→            "id asc",
   162→        ]
   163→    else:
   164→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   165→    explain = {
   166→        "kind": "finding",
   167→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   168→        "confidence": confidence,
   169→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   170→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   171→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   172→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   173→    }
   174→    if is_review:
   175→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   176→    if is_subjective:
   177→        explain["policy"] = (
   178→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   179→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   180→        )
   181→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   182→    return explain
   183→
   184→
   185→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   186→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   187→    for item in items:
   188→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   189→            # Count each member at its own tier
   190→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   191→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   192→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   193→            continue
   194→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   195→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   196→    return counts
   197→
   198→
   199→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   200→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   201→    if not available:
   202→        return None
   203→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   204→
   205→
   206→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   207→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   208→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   209→    for item in items:
   210→        if group == "file":
   211→            key = item.get("file", "")
   212→        elif group == "detector":
   213→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   214→        elif group == "tier":
   215→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   216→        elif group == "cluster":
   217→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   218→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   219→        else:
   220→            key = "items"
   221→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   222→    return grouped
   223→
   224→

   227→    "choose_fallback_tier",

   229→    "item_sort_key",
   230→    "tier_counts",
   231→    "subjective_score_value",
   232→    "group_queue_items",
   233→]
```

```
150→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   151→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   152→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   153→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   154→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   155→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   156→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   157→
   158→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   159→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   160→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   161→
   162→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   163→{
   164→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   165→  "epics": [
   166→    {
   167→      "name": "slug-name",
   168→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   169→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   170→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   171→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   172→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   173→      "agent_safe": true,
   174→      "dependency_order": 1,
   175→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   176→      "status": "pending"
   177→    }
   178→  ],
   179→  "dismissed_findings": [
   180→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   181→  ],
   182→  "contradiction_notes": [
   183→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   184→  ],
   185→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   186→}
   187→"""
   188→
   189→
   190→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   191→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   192→    parts: list[str] = []
   193→
   194→    # Section: existing epics
   195→    if si.existing_epics:
   196→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   197→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   198→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   199→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   200→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   201→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   202→            parts.append(
   203→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   204→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   205→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   206→            )
   207→        parts.append("")
   208→
   209→    # Section: what changed
   210→    if si.new_since_last:
   211→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   212→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   213→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   214→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   215→        parts.append("")
   216→
   217→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   218→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   219→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   220→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   221→        parts.append("")
   222→
   223→    # Section: all open review findings
   224→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   225→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   226→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   227→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   228→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   229→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   230→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   231→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   232→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   233→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   234→        if dimension:
   235→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   236→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   237→        if suggestion:
   238→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   239→    parts.append("")
   240→
   241→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   242→    if si.dimension_scores:
   243→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   244→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   245→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   246→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   247→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   248→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   249→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   250→        parts.append("")
   251→
   252→    # Section: previously dismissed
   253→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   254→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   255→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   256→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   257→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   258→        parts.append("")
   259→
   260→    return "\n".join(parts)
   261→
   262→
   263→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   264→# Result parsing
   265→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   266→
   267→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   268→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   269→
   270→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   271→    """
   272→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   273→
   274→    epics: list[dict] = []
   275→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   276→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   277→            continue
   278→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   279→        if not name:
   280→            continue
   281→        # Validate direction
   282→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   283→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   284→            direction = "simplify"
   285→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   286→        finding_ids = [
   287→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   288→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   289→        ]
   290→        dismissed = [
   291→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   292→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   293→        ]
   294→        action_steps = [
   295→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   296→            if isinstance(s, str)
   297→        ]
   298→
   299→        epics.append({
   300→            "name": name,
   301→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   302→            "direction": direction,
   303→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   304→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   305→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   306→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   307→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   308→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   309→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   310→        })
   311→
   312→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   313→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   314→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   315→            continue
   316→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   317→        if fid in valid_ids:
   318→            dismissed_findings.append(
   319→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   320→            )
   321→
   322→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   323→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   324→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   325→            continue
   326→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   327→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   328→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   329→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   330→        ))
   331→
   332→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   333→
   334→    return SynthesisResult(
   335→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   336→        epics=epics,
   337→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   338→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   339→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   340→    )
   341→
   342→
   343→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   344→# Plan mutation
   345→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   346→
   347→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   348→    plan: PlanModel,
   349→    state: StateModel,
   350→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   351→    *,
   352→    trigger: str = "manual",
   353→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   354→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   355→
   356→    1. Creates/updates epic entries in plan["epics"]
   357→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   358→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   359→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   360→    """
   361→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   362→    now = utc_now()
   363→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   364→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   365→
   366→    epics = plan["epics"]
   367→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   368→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   369→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   370→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   371→    result.synthesis_version = version
   372→
   373→    # --- Update/create epics ------------------------------------------------
   374→    seen_epic_names: set[str] = set()
   375→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   376→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   377→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   378→        seen_epic_names.add(epic_name)
   379→
   380→        existing = epics.get(epic_name)
   381→        if existing:
   382→            # Update existing epic
   383→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   384→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   385→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   386→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   387→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   388→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   389→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   390→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   391→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   392→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   393→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   394→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   395→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   396→            result.epics_updated += 1
   397→        else:
   398→            # Create new epic
   399→            epic: Epic = {
   400→                "name": epic_name,
   401→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   402→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   403→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   404→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   405→                "supersedes": [],
   406→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   407→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   408→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   409→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   410→                "source_clusters": [],
   411→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   412→                "created_at": now,
   413→                "updated_at": now,
   414→                "synthesis_version": version,
   415→                "auto": True,
   416→            }
   417→            epics[epic_name] = epic
   418→            result.epics_created += 1
   419→
   420→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   421→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   422→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   423→        fid = df.finding_id
   424→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   425→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   426→        if fid in order:
   427→            order.remove(fid)
   428→        skipped[fid] = {
   429→            "finding_id": fid,
   430→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   431→            "reason": df.reason,
   432→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   433→            "attestation": None,
   434→            "created_at": now,
   435→            "review_after": None,
   436→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   437→        }
   438→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   439→
   440→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   441→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   442→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   443→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   444→                order.remove(fid)
   445→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   446→                skipped[fid] = {
   447→                    "finding_id": fid,
   448→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   449→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   450→                    "note": None,
   451→                    "attestation": None,
   452→                    "created_at": now,
   453→                    "review_after": None,
   454→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   455→                }
   456→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   457→
   458→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   459→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   460→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   461→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   462→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   463→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   464→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   465→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   466→
   467→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   468→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   469→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   470→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   471→    new_order: list[str] = []
   472→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   473→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   474→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   475→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   476→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   477→    order.clear()
   478→    order.extend(new_order)
   479→
   480→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   481→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   482→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   483→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   484→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   485→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   486→    )
   487→
   488→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   489→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   490→        "last_run": now,
   491→        "version": version,
   492→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   493→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   494→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   495→        "trigger": trigger,
   496→    }
   497→    plan["updated"] = now
   498→
   499→    return result
   500→
   501→
   502→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   503→# Top-level orchestrator
   504→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   505→
   506→@dataclass
   507→class SynthesisDeps:
   508→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   509→
   510→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   511→
   512→
   513→def synthesize_epics(
   514→    plan: PlanModel,
   515→    state: StateModel,
   516→    *,
   517→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   518→    dry_run: bool = False,
   519→    trigger: str = "manual",
   520→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   521→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   522→
   523→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   524→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   525→
   526→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   527→    """
   528→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   529→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   530→
   531→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   532→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   533→
   534→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   535→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   536→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   537→        return result
   538→
   539→    # Call LLM
   540→    try:
   541→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   542→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   543→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   544→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   545→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   546→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   547→        return result
   548→
   549→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
```

```
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→

    41→    else:
```

```
    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    26→    plan = load_plan()
    27→    try:

    29→    except ValueError as ex:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    31→        return
    32→    save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    39→        return

    41→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")

    44→    plan = load_plan()

    51→    except ValueError as ex:

    53→        return

    57→

    96→
    97→    plan = load_plan()
    98→
    99→    offset: int | None = None
   100→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   101→        try:

   104→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))

   106→        target = None
   107→
   108→    try:

   110→    except ValueError as ex:

   112→        return
   113→    save_plan(plan)

   117→def _print_cluster_member(

   119→) -> None:

   127→    if file:

   135→        if override.get("note"):

   148→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
```

```
1→"""Plan move subcommand handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→

     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.plan._resolve import resolve_ids_from_patterns

    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, move_items, save_plan

    13→
    14→def cmd_plan_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    15→    """Move findings to a position in the queue."""
    16→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    17→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    18→        return
    19→
    20→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    21→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
    22→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
    23→
    24→    plan = load_plan()
    25→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    26→    if not finding_ids:
    27→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    offset: int | None = None
    31→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
    32→        try:
    33→            offset = int(target)
    34→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
    35→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
    36→            return
    37→        target = None
    38→
    39→    count = move_items(plan, finding_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
    40→    save_plan(plan)
    41→    print(colorize(f"  Moved {count} item(s) to {position}.", "green"))
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
```

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Desloppify Plan Queue Rendering and Finding ID Handling

I've thoroughly explored the desloppify plan queue rendering system and finding ID handling mechanisms. Here are my detailed findings:

### 1. QUEUE TABLE RENDERING (queue_render.py)

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`

**Table Structure:**
- **Headers:** `["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]` (lines 108)
- **Column Widths:** `[4, 4, 12, 44, 24]` (line 152)
- **Display Items Limit:** Default `top=30`, can be set to 0 for all (line 39)

**Key Observations:**
- **Finding IDs are NOT displayed in the table** — only position number (#), tier, detector, summary, and cluster name
- Position uses sequential numbering (1-indexed) from `enumerate(display_items)` (line 110)
- Summary is truncated to 48 characters using `_truncate()` function (line 148)
- Plan cluster name is shown in the "Cluster" column when applicable (line 142)
- Skipped items append a `[skip]` suffix to the summary (line 146)

**Item Types Rendered:**
1. **synthesis_needed** (kind): T1 synthetic item, detector="synthesis", summary shows finding count
2. **epic** (kind): T1 synthetic item, detector="epic", summary includes direction, count, status
3. **cluster** (kind): Meta-item with member count, uses tier range (T2-T3) or single tier
4. **finding** (default): Regular findings with tier, detector, summary

**Skipped Items Handling:**
- `include_skipped` flag controls whether skipped items appear (line 42, 123)
- If `--include-skipped` is omitted and items are skipped, a note suggests using it (lines 88-96)
- Skipped items from plan are counted separately and appended at the end of results (line 124 in core.py)

### 2. FINDING ID FORMATS AND PATTERNS (_matches_pattern in filtering.py)

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py`

**Finding ID Structure:**
- Format: `{detector}::{file}::{name}` (without name: `{detector}::{file}`)
- Example: `python_unused::src/foo.py::unused_var`
- Created in `make_finding()` function (lines 141-169)

**Pattern Matching Rules (_matches_pattern, lines 172-183):**
1. **Exact match:** `finding_id == pattern`
2. **Glob patterns:** `fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern)` if `*` in pattern
3. **Prefix match:** `finding_id.startswith(pattern)` if `::` in pattern
4. **Detector match:** `finding.get("detector") == pattern`
5. **File path match:** Exact or prefix-based file path matching

**Hash-Based Short ID Support (scope_matches in helpers.py, lines 92-94):**
```python
# Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
```
- Pattern: 8+ hexadecimal characters
- Usage: Matches the last segment after `::` in finding ID
- Example: `8a3f5c2d` would match `detector::file::8a3f5c2d`

**Pattern Resolution (resolve_ids_from_patterns in _resolve.py):**
- Uses `match_findings()` which internally calls `_matches_pattern()`
- Falls back to literal plan ID matching if pattern exists in plan but not state (lines 42-45)
- Supports `status_filter` parameter: `"open"` or `"all"` (line 35)
- Returns deduplicated list of finding IDs (line 46)

  - `"after"` + target: after target ID
  - `"up"` + offset: move up N positions
  - `"down"` + offset: move down N positions

### 4. CLUSTER DELETE OPERATION (cluster_handlers.py + operations.py)

```python
def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    plan = load_plan()
    try:
        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)  # Returns list of orphaned IDs
    except ValueError as ex:

        return

```

**Delete Operation (operations.py, lines 337-355):**
- Removes cluster from `plan["clusters"]` dict
- Returns list of orphaned finding IDs
- Clears cluster references from `overrides` for each member (lines 346-350)
- Clears `active_cluster` if focused on deleted cluster (lines 352-353)
- **Important:** Does NOT remove IDs from `queue_order` or `skipped` — items remain in queue orphaned

**Reconciliation (reconcile.py):**
- **No automatic re-creation of deleted clusters**
- Reconciliation runs after scan save (scan_workflow.py) as best-effort
- Superseded entries (items no longer in state) are marked but not resurfaced
- Auto-clusters are regenerated during `auto_cluster_findings()` if conditions match

### 5. UNSKIP AND QUEUE POSITION RESTORATION (override_handlers.py + operations.py)

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py`

**Unskip Handler (lines 173-206):**
```python
def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    plan = load_plan()
    # Resolve patterns with status_filter="all" to match skipped items
    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
    
    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
    save_plan(plan)
```

**Unskip Operation (operations.py, lines 162-183):**
```python
def unskip_items(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    """Returns (count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)"""
    count = 0
    need_reopen: list[str] = []
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
    for fid in finding_ids:
        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
        if entry is not None:
            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
                need_reopen.append(fid)
            # RESTORES TO END OF QUEUE
            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
            count += 1
    return count, need_reopen
```

**Queue Position Restoration:**
- **Items are restored to the END of `queue_order`**, not original position
- No position history is tracked
- Permanent/false_positive skips also reopen in state layer
- Success message: `"Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue."` (line 205)

### 6. INCLUDE-SKIPPED IMPLEMENTATION (queue_render.py + core.py)

**Location:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

**Flag Usage:**
- Command-line: `--include-skipped` flag (line 42 in queue_render.py)
- Passed to `QueueBuildOptions(include_skipped=...)` (line 65)
- Used in `_apply_plan_order()` (lines 59-145)

**Queue Assembly Logic (core.py, lines 59-145):**
```python
def _apply_plan_order(
    items: list[dict],
    plan: dict,
    *,
    include_skipped: bool = False,
    cluster: str | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
    # Build three lists:
    ordered: list[dict] = []  # Items in queue_order (not skipped)
    remaining: list[dict] = []  # Other items not in queue_order
    skipped_items: list[dict] = []  # Items in skipped dict
    
    # Assemble result
    result = ordered + remaining
    if include_skipped:
        result = result + skipped_items  # Append skipped at end
    
    # Annotate with plan metadata (descriptions, notes, cluster info)
    # Apply cluster filter if specified
```

**Item Annotation:**
- Each item gets `plan_skipped=True` if in skipped dict (line 129)
- Skip kind, reason stored in item (lines 132-135)
- Cluster membership annotated with `plan_cluster` dict (lines 92-100)

**Header Display Logic (queue_render.py, lines 74-96):**

```python
non_skipped = total - skipped_count

# If items are skipped but not shown in output:
if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    if plan_skipped_count:
        print(f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s'...} hidden — use --include-skipped)")
```

**Primary Finding ID Matching:**

2. **Prefix:** `detector::file` (without name)
3. **Detector wildcard:** `detector::*`
4. **File path:** Full or prefix path
5. **Detector name only:** Matches all findings from that detector

**Subjective Dimension Aliases (helpers.py, lines 118-128):**

- Example: "Mid elegance" → aliases include `mid_elegance`, `mid elegance`, `midelegan...`

## Comprehensive Exploration Report: Desloppify Plan Focus Mode & Reconciliation System

Based on my deep dive into the codebase, here are the answers to your five key questions:

---

### 1. **How Focus Mode Filters the Queue in `next`**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

Focus mode filtering happens in `_apply_plan_order()` (lines 59-145):

- **Active cluster lookup** (line 78): `active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")`
- **Cluster filtering logic** (lines 137-143):
  ```python
  effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
  if effective_cluster:
      cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
      cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
      if cluster_member_ids:
          result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
  ```

**How it works:**
- When `plan.active_cluster` is set (via `plan focus <name>`), the queue is filtered to show ONLY findings that are members of that cluster
- The filter respects the plan ordering: items appear in `queue_order` position first, then remaining items in mechanical sort order
- Epic focus creates a **transient cluster entry** so the filter can match epic finding_ids (see `set_focus()` in operations.py, lines 382-405)
- **Collapse logic** (lines 416-423): Auto-clusters are NOT collapsed when `active_cluster` is set, showing individual findings instead of meta-items

**Rendering:** In `render.py` lines 475-491, the `next` command shows a "Focused on" header when active_cluster is set, displaying the cluster name and progress through members.

---

### 2. **How Reconciliation Works & Why Clusters Recreate**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`

Reconciliation runs **post-scan** and handles finding churn:

**Key behaviors:**

1. **Finding expiration** (lines 138-165): Collects all IDs referenced in the plan (queue_order, skipped, clusters, overrides, epics) and marks as "superseded" any that:
   - No longer exist in state.findings
   - Are no longer open (status != "open")
   - Are already superseded or synthetic (SYNTHESIS_ID, subjective:: prefix)

2. **Cleanup actions**:
   - Removes from queue_order, skipped, cluster memberships (lines 85-95)
   - Creates SupersededEntry tracking original detector, file, candidates for remapping
   - Stores override notes for history

3. **Stale skip resurfacing** (lines 167-174): Re-surfaces temporary skips past their `review_after` threshold

4. **Old superseded pruning** (lines 176-178): Removes superseded entries older than 90 days (SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)

5. **Epic cleanup** (lines 181-198): Removes dead finding_ids from epics, marks completed when all findings resolved, deletes empty epics

**Why clusters recreate:** This is NOT handled by reconcile. Instead, **auto-clustering regeneration** happens via `auto_cluster_findings()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` (lines 223-470):

- Runs independently of reconciliation (typically on each scan)
- Rebuilds auto-clusters from current open findings (lines 240-341)
- **Key insight:** User-modified clusters (`user_modified=True`) preserve their membership and only merge in NEW findings (lines 294-301)
- **Non-user-modified clusters** get wholesale replacement if members changed (lines 302-311)
- **Stale auto-clusters are deleted** if their grouping key has no active matches (lines 438-467)

**The "cluster delete doesn't stick" issue likely stems from:**
- If a cluster is auto-generated and not marked `user_modified`, it gets **recreated at the next auto_cluster_findings() call** when findings match its grouping key again
- Only **user-created clusters** (not starting with "auto/") persist through auto-clustering
- Deleting an auto-cluster is essentially a soft-delete; it reappears when findings match its key pattern

---

### 3. **How `next` Command Renders Items & Epic Card Skipping**

**Files:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`

**Epic rendering** (lines 420-459):
- Epic items are built as synthetic queue items (kind="epic") with:
  - `id`: epic/<name>
  - `tier`: 1 (always T1)
  - Summary: `[direction] thesis`
  - Detail: thesis, direction, root_cause, status, agent_safe, dependency_order, finding_ids, action_steps
- Rendered via `_render_epic_item()` showing:
  - Thesis, root cause, status, epic count in meta-plan
  - List of first 5 finding_ids (with "...N more" if larger)
  - Action: `desloppify plan focus <epic_name>` to drill in

**Epic inclusion in queue** (lines 187-229):
- Epics are included if they are NOT completed (`epic.status != "completed"`)
- Each epic must have at least one finding_id to be included
- Injected into all_items via `_inject_synthesis_and_epic_items()` (lines 232-253)

**Epic skipping behavior:**
- **Epic cards CANNOT be skipped directly** — there's no mechanism in the code to skip an epic item itself
- Instead, users should:
  - Mark individual findings as done/skipped via `plan done` or `plan skip`
  - The epic remains until all its findings are resolved (reconciliation deletes empty epics)
  - Or focus on the epic (`plan focus epic/<name>`) then work through findings one by one
- **Synthesis dismissal** (epic_synthesis.py lines 420-456): Findings can be marked as "synthesized_out" during epic synthesis, which removes them from the queue entirely

---

### 4. **Mechanisms for Permanently Removing Issues from the Plan**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`

**Three permanent removal mechanisms:**

1. **`skip_items()` with kind="permanent"** (lines 130-159):
   - Moves findings to `plan.skipped` dict with `kind="permanent"`
   - Requires `note` (wontfix reason) and optional `attestation`
   - Finding is hidden from queue permanently until explicitly unskipped
   - Removal is **plan-layer only** — finding still exists in state

2. **`purge_ids()` (lines 431-459):**
   - **Removes finding IDs entirely from the plan**
   - Cleans from queue_order, skipped dict, and all cluster memberships
   - Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes kept for history)
   - Returns count of IDs that were actually present
   - **Most aggressive option** — total erasure from plan structures

3. **Epic synthesis dismissal** (epic_synthesis.py lines 420-456):
   - LLM marks findings as `"synthesized_out"` skip entries
   - Removes from queue_order, adds to skipped with kind="synthesized_out"
   - Per-epic dismissed lists also trigger this (lines 440-456)
   - Marked as dismissed by epic synthesis vN with timestamp

**No "dismiss" subcommand exists**, but the workflow is:
```bash
desloppify plan skip --permanent "<finding_id>" --note "why" --attest "evidence"
```

---

### 5. **Plan Data Model for Dismissed/Removed Items**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

**Key fields in PlanModel** (lines 94-106):

```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict):
    queue_order: list[str]              # Prioritized active findings
    deferred: list[str]                 # v1 compat (migrated → skipped)
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]       # Skipped findings with metadata
    active_cluster: str | None          # Current focus cluster
    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]  # Descriptions, notes, cluster membership
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]        # Both manual and auto-clusters
    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]  # Findings that disappeared
    epics: dict[str, Epic]              # LLM-synthesized meta-plan
    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta  # Synthesis version, dismissed_ids, hash
```

**SkipEntry fields** (lines 21-29):
```python
kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive" | "synthesized_out"
reason: str | None   # Why (short form)
note: str | None     # Required for permanent (wontfix explanation)
attestation: str | None  # Evidence URL or justification
created_at: str
review_after: int | None  # Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
skipped_at_scan: int  # State scan_count when skipped
```

**Epic fields include dismissed tracking** (lines 53-70):
```python
dismissed: list[str]  # Finding IDs dismissed by this epic
status: str  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
```

**SupersededEntry fields** (lines 82-91):
```python
status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
superseded_at: str
remapped_to: str | None  # If finding was remapped to a new ID
candidates: list[str]  # Possible remaps (up to 5)
note: str | None  # Preserved override note
```

**EpicSynthesisMeta fields** (lines 72-79):
```python
dismissed_ids: list[str]  # IDs dismissed by synthesis (including per-epic dismissed)
synthesized_ids: list[str]  # IDs that were included in synthesis
version: int  # Synthesis version number
finding_snapshot_hash: str  # Hash of review findings snapshot
```

---

## Summary of Key Insights

| Aspect | Finding |
|--------|---------|
| **Focus mode** | Filters queue to cluster members; active_cluster field controls it. Epic focus creates transient cluster entries. |
| **Cluster recreation** | Auto-clusters are deleted but **regenerate** at next auto_cluster_findings() if findings match their grouping key. Only user-created clusters (non-auto/) persist. |
| **Reconciliation** | Happens post-scan; marks disappeared findings as superseded, removes from plan structures, prunes old entries (90-day TTL), cleans up empty epics. |
| **Epic cards** | Cannot be skipped directly. Mark findings done/skipped individually. Epics auto-delete when empty. |
| **Permanent removal** | Use `plan skip --permanent` (plan-layer) or `purge_ids()` (total plan erasure). Epic synthesis marks as `synthesized_out`. |
| **Data model** | PlanModel tracks dismissed via multiple fields: skipped.kind, superseded.status, epic_synthesis_meta.dismissed_ids, and per-epic dismissed lists. |

Design an implementation plan for fixing the top UX issues found by 5 test agents exercising the desloppify plan system. The goal is to fix the bugs and the highest-impact friction points.

## Bugs to fix (3)

### Bug 1: `cluster delete` doesn't stick for auto-clusters
**Root cause:** Auto-clusters are regenerated by `auto_cluster_findings()` on each scan. Deleting an auto-cluster removes it, but it gets recreated when findings match its grouping key. The `_cmd_cluster_delete` in `cluster_handlers.py` calls `delete_cluster()` in `operations.py` and `save_plan()`, which works — but the next auto-clustering pass recreates it.
**Fix:** In `auto_cluster.py`, the function `auto_cluster_findings()` should check if a cluster was explicitly deleted by the user. Add a `deleted_clusters: list[str]` field to the plan schema, and skip regenerating clusters whose names are in that list.
**Files:** `engine/_plan/schema.py` (add field), `engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` (check list), `engine/_plan/operations.py` `delete_cluster()` (add to list)

### Bug 2: Skip → unskip loses queue position
**Root cause:** In `operations.py` `unskip_items()`, unskipped items are appended to the END of `queue_order` with `plan["queue_order"].append(fid)`. No position history is tracked.
**Fix:** When skipping an item, record its queue position in the SkipEntry. When unskipping, restore to original position (or nearest valid position). 
**Files:** `engine/_plan/operations.py` `skip_items()` (save position) and `unskip_items()` (restore position), `engine/_plan/schema.py` (add `original_position: int | None` to SkipEntry)

### Bug 3: `--include-skipped` shows nothing
**Root cause:** The skipped items in `core.py` `_apply_plan_order()` are only included if they appear in the `items` list passed in. But the items list comes from the work queue builder which already excludes skipped items from state. The 8038 false-positive skips exist only in the plan's `skipped` dict and have no corresponding work-queue item to display.
**Fix:** When `include_skipped=True`, build synthetic display items for plan-skipped entries that aren't in the items list. Use the finding data from state to fill in detector/summary/tier.
**Files:** `engine/_work_queue/core.py` `_apply_plan_order()` — build synthetic items from `plan["skipped"]` + `state["findings"]`

## Top UX improvements (prioritized)

### 4. Add finding IDs to queue table
Every agent complained about not being able to reference items from the queue. Add a truncated ID column or append the 8-char hash suffix to the summary.
**Approach:** Append the 8-char hash suffix to each summary line in queue_render.py. This is minimal and doesn't need a new column. Format: `"summary text... [abc12345]"`. The hash suffix already works as a pattern in `_matches_pattern` via `scope_matches()` in helpers.py.
**File:** `app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` — modify summary_display construction

### 5. Improve move confirmation messages
"Moved to after" → "Moved to after delete-dead-facades"
**File:** `app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` `_cmd_cluster_move()` line 114 — include the target name in the message.

### 6. Focus silent shift warning  
When skip/done empties the focused cluster, warn the user instead of silently showing a different cluster.
**Approach:** In `render_terminal_items()` in render.py, after the focus header block, check if the focused cluster has 0 items in the current view and print a warning.
**File:** `app/commands/next_parts/render.py`

### 7. Suppress `File: .` for holistic findings
Holistic review findings show `File: .` which is noise. Suppress it in the next/show renderers.
**Approach:** In the render functions, skip printing the file line when `file == "."`.
**Files:** `app/commands/next_parts/render.py` (in `_render_item`), `app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` (in `_print_cluster_member`)

### 8. Better "no matching findings" error
Currently just says "No matching findings found." with no guidance. Add a hint about pattern formats.
**File:** Multiple files that use this message — but the most important one is `app/commands/plan/_resolve.py` or the callers in override_handlers.py, cluster_handlers.py. Best approach: create a helper function that prints the message + pattern format hints, and call it from all the "no match" sites.

### 9. Add `--dry-run` to skip
Preview what would be matched before committing.
**File:** `app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` `cmd_plan_skip()` — add a `--dry-run` flag that resolves patterns, prints the matched IDs, and returns without modifying state.

## Items to NOT tackle (low impact or out of scope)
- Queue count fluctuation — this is inherent to reconciliation running post-scan; documenting it is better than fixing it
- `cluster list --manual` filter — nice but low priority
- `cluster rename` / `cluster merge` — feature additions, not UX fixes
- Score impact per item — requires significant computation changes
- `next --actionable` — epics are intentional coaching; agents should learn to use focus

## Implementation order
1. Bug 2 (skip position) — highest workflow impact, isolated change
2. Item 4 (hash IDs in queue) — universal #1 friction point
3. Item 5 (move confirmations) — trivial fix
4. Bug 1 (cluster delete) — important but auto-clusters are less common
5. Item 7 (suppress File: .) — quick visual improvement  
6. Item 6 (focus empty warning) — small targeted change
7. Item 8 (no-match hints) — moderate effort, touches multiple files
8. Bug 3 (include-skipped) — complex, lower priority since FP skips are edge case
9. Item 9 (skip dry-run) — nice to have

Provide detailed implementation guidance for each item including exact code locations and patterns.

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 3
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = frozenset({
    14→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    15→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→

    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→
    31→
    32→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    33→    finding_id: Required[str]

    35→    note: str | None
    36→    cluster: str | None
    37→    created_at: str
    38→    updated_at: str
    39→
    40→

    44→    finding_ids: list[str]

    46→    updated_at: str

    57→    root_cause: str
    58→    finding_ids: list[str]

    61→    agent_safe: bool
    62→    dependency_order: int

    94→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    95→    version: Required[int]
    96→    created: Required[str]
    97→    updated: Required[str]
    98→    queue_order: list[str]
    99→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   100→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   101→    active_cluster: str | None
   102→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   103→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   104→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   105→    epics: dict[str, Epic]
   106→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   107→
   108→
   109→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   110→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   111→    now = utc_now()
   112→    return {
   113→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   114→        "created": now,
   115→        "updated": now,
   116→        "queue_order": [],
   117→        "deferred": [],
   118→        "skipped": {},
   119→        "active_cluster": None,
   120→        "overrides": {},
   121→        "clusters": {},
   122→        "superseded": {},
   123→        "epics": {},
   124→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   125→    }
   126→
   127→
   128→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   129→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   130→
   131→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
   132→    """
   133→    defaults = empty_plan()
   134→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   135→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   136→
   137→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   138→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   139→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   140→        plan["deferred"] = []
   141→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   142→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   143→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   144→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   145→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   146→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   147→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   148→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   149→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epics"), dict):
   150→        plan["epics"] = {}
   151→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   152→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   153→
   154→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   155→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   156→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   157→    if deferred:
   158→        now = utc_now()
   159→        for fid in list(deferred):
   160→            if fid not in skipped:
   161→                skipped[fid] = {
   162→                    "finding_id": fid,
   163→                    "kind": "temporary",
   164→                    "reason": None,
   165→                    "note": None,
   166→                    "attestation": None,
   167→                    "created_at": now,
   168→                    "review_after": None,
   169→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   170→                }
   171→        deferred.clear()
   172→
   173→    # Normalize cluster fields
   174→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   175→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   176→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   177→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   178→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   179→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   180→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   181→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   182→
   183→
   184→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   185→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   186→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   187→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   188→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   189→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   190→
   191→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   192→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   193→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   194→    if overlap:
   195→        raise ValueError(
   196→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   197→        )
   198→
   199→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   200→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   201→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   202→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   203→            raise ValueError(
   204→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   205→            )
   206→
   207→
   208→__all__ = [
   209→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   210→    "Epic",
   211→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   212→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   213→    "Cluster",
   214→    "ItemOverride",
   215→    "PlanModel",
   216→    "SkipEntry",
   217→    "SupersededEntry",
   218→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   219→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   220→    "empty_plan",
   221→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   222→    "validate_plan",
   223→]
   224→
```
