1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    PlanModel,
     8→    SkipEntry,
     9→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    10→)
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    12→
    13→
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→# Helpers
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→
    18→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    19→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    20→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    21→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    22→    if finding_id in order:
    23→        order.remove(finding_id)
    24→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    25→
    26→
    27→def _resolve_position(
    28→    order: list[str],
    29→    position: str,
    30→    target: str | None = None,
    31→    offset: int | None = None,
    32→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    33→) -> int:
    34→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    35→
    36→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    37→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    38→    """
    39→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    40→
    41→    if position == "top":
    42→        return 0
    43→    if position == "bottom":
    44→        return len(order)
    45→
    46→    if position == "before" and target:
    47→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    48→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    49→                return i
    50→        return 0
    51→
    52→    if position == "after" and target:
    53→        for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
    54→            if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
    55→                return i + 1
    56→        return len(order)
    57→
    58→    if position == "up" and offset is not None:
    59→        if not finding_ids:
    60→            return 0
    61→        # Find current position of first item
    62→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    63→        current_idx = None
    64→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    65→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    66→            if item_id == first_id:
    67→                current_idx = i
    68→                break
    69→        if current_idx is None:
    70→            # Item not yet in order; treat as "insert at position offset from top"
    71→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
    72→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
    73→
    74→    if position == "down" and offset is not None:
    75→        if not finding_ids:
    76→            return len(order)
    77→        first_id = finding_ids[0]
    78→        clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
    79→        current_idx = None
    80→        for i, item_id in enumerate(clean_order):
    81→            if item_id == first_id:
    82→                current_idx = i
    83→                break
    84→        if current_idx is None:
    85→            return len(clean_order)
    86→        return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
    87→
    88→    return len(order)
    89→
    90→
    91→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    92→# Move items
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→
    95→def move_items(
    96→    plan: PlanModel,
    97→    finding_ids: list[str],
    98→    position: str,
    99→    target: str | None = None,
   100→    offset: int | None = None,
   101→) -> int:
   102→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   103→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   104→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   105→
   106→    # Remove from skipped if present
   107→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   108→    for fid in finding_ids:
   109→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   110→
   111→    # Remove from current position in order
   112→    for fid in finding_ids:
   113→        if fid in order:
   114→            order.remove(fid)
   115→
   116→    # Resolve insertion point
   117→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   118→
   119→    # Insert in original order
   120→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   121→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   122→
   123→    return len(finding_ids)
   124→
   125→
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→# Skip / unskip
   128→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   129→
   130→def skip_items(
   131→    plan: PlanModel,
   132→    finding_ids: list[str],
   133→    *,
   134→    kind: str = "temporary",
   135→    reason: str | None = None,
   136→    note: str | None = None,
   137→    attestation: str | None = None,
   138→    review_after: int | None = None,
   139→    scan_count: int = 0,
   140→) -> int:
   141→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    count = 0
   145→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   146→    for fid in finding_ids:
   147→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   148→        skipped[fid] = {
   149→            "finding_id": fid,
   150→            "kind": kind,
   151→            "reason": reason,
   152→            "note": note,
   153→            "attestation": attestation,
   154→            "created_at": now,
   155→            "review_after": review_after,
   156→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   157→        }
   158→        count += 1
   159→    return count
   160→
   161→
   162→def unskip_items(
   163→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   164→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   165→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   166→
   167→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   168→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   169→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   170→    """
   171→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   172→    count = 0
   173→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   174→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   177→        if entry is not None:
   178→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   179→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   180→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   181→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   182→            count += 1
   183→    return count, need_reopen
   184→
   185→
   186→def resurface_stale_skips(
   187→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   188→) -> list[str]:
   189→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   190→
   191→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   192→    """
   193→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   194→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   195→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   196→    for fid in list(skipped):
   197→        entry = skipped[fid]
   198→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   199→            continue
   200→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   201→        if review_after is None:
   202→            continue
   203→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   204→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   205→            skipped.pop(fid)
   206→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   207→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   208→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   209→    return resurfaced
   210→
   211→
   212→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   213→# Describe / annotate
   214→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   215→
   216→def describe_finding(
   217→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   218→) -> None:
   219→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   220→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   221→    now = utc_now()
   222→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   223→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   224→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   225→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   226→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   227→
   228→
   229→def annotate_finding(
   230→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   231→) -> None:
   232→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   233→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   234→    now = utc_now()
   235→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   236→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   237→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   238→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   239→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   240→
   241→
   242→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   243→# Clusters
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→
   246→def create_cluster(
   247→    plan: PlanModel,
   248→    name: str,
   249→    description: str | None = None,
   250→    action: str | None = None,
   251→) -> Cluster:
   252→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   253→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   254→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   255→        raise ValueError(
   256→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   257→        )
   258→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   259→        raise ValueError(
   260→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for epics: {name!r}"
   261→        )
   262→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   263→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   264→    now = utc_now()
   265→    cluster: Cluster = {
   266→        "name": name,
   267→        "description": description,
   268→        "finding_ids": [],
   269→        "created_at": now,
   270→        "updated_at": now,
   271→        "auto": False,
   272→        "cluster_key": "",
   273→        "action": action,
   274→        "user_modified": False,
   275→    }
   276→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   277→    return cluster
   278→
   279→
   280→def add_to_cluster(
   281→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   282→) -> int:
   283→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   284→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   285→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   286→    if cluster is None:
   287→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   288→
   289→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   290→    count = 0
   291→    now = utc_now()
   292→    for fid in finding_ids:
   293→        if fid not in member_ids:
   294→            member_ids.append(fid)
   295→            count += 1
   296→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   297→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   298→        if fid not in overrides:
   299→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   300→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   301→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   302→
   303→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   304→    return count
   305→
   306→
   307→def remove_from_cluster(
   308→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   309→) -> int:
   310→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   311→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   312→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   313→    if cluster is None:
   314→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   315→
   316→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   317→    now = utc_now()
   318→    count = 0
   319→    for fid in finding_ids:
   320→        if fid in member_ids:
   321→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   322→            count += 1
   323→        # Clear cluster from override
   324→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   325→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   326→            override["cluster"] = None
   327→            override["updated_at"] = now
   328→
   329→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   330→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   331→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   332→
   333→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   334→    return count
   335→
   336→
   337→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   338→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   339→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   340→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   341→    if cluster is None:
   342→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   343→
   344→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   345→    now = utc_now()
   346→    for fid in orphaned:
   347→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   348→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   349→            override["cluster"] = None
   350→            override["updated_at"] = now
   351→
   352→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   353→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   354→
   355→    return orphaned
   356→
   357→
   358→def move_cluster(
   359→    plan: PlanModel,
   360→    cluster_name: str,
   361→    position: str,
   362→    target: str | None = None,
   363→    offset: int | None = None,
   364→) -> int:
   365→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   366→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   367→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   368→    if cluster is None:
   369→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   370→
   371→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   372→    if not member_ids:
   373→        return 0
   374→
   375→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   376→
   377→
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→# Focus
   380→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   381→
   382→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   383→    """Set the active cluster focus.
   384→
   385→    Supports epic/ names — creates a transient cluster entry so the
   386→    queue filter can match epic finding_ids.
   387→    """
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    if cluster_name.startswith("epic/"):
   390→        epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   391→        if cluster_name not in epics:
   392→            raise ValueError(f"Epic {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   393→        epic = epics[cluster_name]
   394→        # Create transient cluster entry for queue filtering
   395→        plan["clusters"][cluster_name] = {
   396→            "name": cluster_name,
   397→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   398→            "finding_ids": list(epic.get("finding_ids", [])),
   399→            "auto": True,
   400→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{cluster_name}",
   401→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {cluster_name}",
   402→            "user_modified": False,
   403→        }
   404→        plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   405→        return
   406→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   407→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   408→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   409→
   410→
   411→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   412→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   413→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   414→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   415→
   416→
   417→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   418→# Reset
   419→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   420→
   421→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   422→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   423→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   424→    plan.clear()
   425→    from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import empty_plan as _empty
   426→    for k, v in _empty().items():
   427→        plan[k] = v
   428→    plan["created"] = created
   429→
   430→
   431→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   432→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   433→
   434→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   435→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   436→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   437→    """
   438→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   439→    found = 0
   440→
   441→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   442→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   443→    for fid in finding_ids:
   444→        was_present = False
   445→        if fid in order:
   446→            order.remove(fid)
   447→            was_present = True
   448→        if fid in skipped:
   449→            skipped.pop(fid)
   450→            was_present = True
   451→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   452→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   453→            if fid in ids:
   454→                ids.remove(fid)
   455→                was_present = True
   456→        if was_present:
   457→            found += 1
   458→
   459→    return found
   460→
   461→
   462→__all__ = [
   463→    "add_to_cluster",
   464→    "annotate_finding",
   465→    "clear_focus",
   466→    "create_cluster",
   467→    "delete_cluster",
   468→    "describe_finding",
   469→    "move_cluster",
   470→    "move_items",
   471→    "purge_ids",
   472→    "remove_from_cluster",
   473→    "reset_plan",
   474→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   475→    "set_focus",
   476→    "skip_items",
   477→    "unskip_items",
   478→]
   479→

    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None
    34→    status: str = "open"
    35→    include_subjective: bool = True

    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→) -> list[dict]:
    66→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    67→
    68→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    69→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    70→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    71→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    72→    """
    73→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    74→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    75→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    76→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    77→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    78→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    79→
    80→    # Build lookup
    81→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    82→    for item in items:
    83→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    84→
    85→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    86→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    87→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    88→        if override.get("description"):
    89→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    90→        if override.get("note"):
    91→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    92→        if override.get("cluster"):
    93→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    94→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    95→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    96→                "name": cluster_name,
    97→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    98→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    99→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   100→            }
   101→
   102→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   103→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   104→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   105→    for fid in queue_order:
   106→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   107→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   108→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   109→
   110→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   111→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   112→    for item in items:
   113→        item_id = item["id"]
   114→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   115→            continue
   116→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   117→            skipped_items.append(item)
   118→        else:
   119→            remaining.append(item)
   120→
   121→    # Assign queue positions
   122→    result = ordered + remaining
   123→    if include_skipped:
   124→        result = result + skipped_items
   125→
   126→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   127→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   128→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   129→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   130→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   131→            if skip_entry:
   132→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   133→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   134→                if skip_reason:
   135→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   136→
   137→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   138→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   139→    if effective_cluster:
   140→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   141→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   142→        if cluster_member_ids:
   143→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   144→
   145→    return result
   146→
   147→
   148→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   149→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   150→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   151→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   152→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   153→    if kind == "cluster":
   154→        return any(
   155→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   156→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   157→        )
   158→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   159→
   160→
   161→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   162→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   163→    try:
   164→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   165→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   166→        if meta:
   167→            return meta.action_type
   168→    except ImportError:
   169→        pass
   170→    return "manual_fix"
   171→
   172→
   173→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   174→
   175→
   176→def _epic_membership(epics: dict) -> dict[str, str]:
   177→    """Build mapping of finding id to epic name."""
   178→    finding_to_epic: dict[str, str] = {}
   179→    for epic_name, epic in epics.items():
   180→        if epic.get("status") == "completed":
   181→            continue
   182→        for finding_id in epic.get("finding_ids", []):
   183→            finding_to_epic[finding_id] = epic_name
   184→    return finding_to_epic
   185→
   186→
   187→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   188→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress epic."""
   189→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   190→    if not epics:
   191→        return []
   192→
   193→    total_epics = len(epics)
   194→    items: list[dict] = []
   195→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   196→        epics.items(),
   197→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   198→    ):
   199→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   200→        if status == "completed":
   201→            continue
   202→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   203→        if not finding_ids:
   204→            continue
   205→
   206→        items.append({
   207→            "id": epic_name,
   208→            "kind": "epic",
   209→            "tier": 1,
   210→            "effective_tier": 1,
   211→            "confidence": "high",
   212→            "detector": "epic",
   213→            "file": ".",
   214→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   215→            "status": "open",
   216→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   217→            "detail": {
   218→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   219→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   220→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   221→                "epic_status": status,
   222→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   223→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   224→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   225→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   226→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   227→            },
   228→        })
   229→    return items
   230→
   231→
   232→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   233→    *,
   234→    all_items: list[dict],
   235→    plan: dict,
   236→    state: StateModel,
   237→    scope: str | None,
   238→) -> None:
   239→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   240→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   241→
   242→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   243→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   244→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   245→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   246→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   247→
   248→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   249→    for item in epic_items:
   250→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   251→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   252→                all_items.append(item)
   253→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   254→
   255→
   256→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   257→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   258→
   259→    Epic membership takes precedence: findings in epics are excluded
   260→    from cluster collapsing (they'll be represented by epic items).
   261→    """
   262→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   263→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   264→
   265→    # Build epic membership to exclude from cluster collapse
   266→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   267→    if epics:
   268→        for epic in epics.values():
   269→            if epic.get("status") != "completed":
   270→                epic_finding_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   271→
   272→    if not clusters:
   273→        return items
   274→
   275→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   276→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   277→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   278→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   279→            continue
   280→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   281→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   282→
   283→    # Remove epic findings from cluster membership
   284→    for fid in epic_finding_ids:
   285→        fid_to_cluster.pop(fid, None)
   286→
   287→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   288→        return items
   289→
   290→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   291→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   292→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   293→
   294→    for item in items:
   295→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   296→        if cname:
   297→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   298→        else:
   299→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   300→
   301→    # Build cluster meta-items
   302→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   303→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   304→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   305→        if len(members) < 2:
   306→            result.extend(members)
   307→            continue
   308→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   309→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   310→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   311→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   312→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   313→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   314→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   315→            action_type = "reorganize"
   316→        else:
   317→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   318→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   319→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   320→                action_type = "refactor"
   321→
   322→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   323→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   324→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   325→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   326→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   327→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   328→        else:
   329→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   330→
   331→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   332→        if not primary_command:
   333→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   334→
   335→        meta_item: dict = {
   336→            "id": cname,
   337→            "kind": "cluster",
   338→            "action_type": action_type,
   339→            "summary": summary,
   340→            "members": members,
   341→            "member_count": len(members),
   342→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   343→            "cluster_name": cname,
   344→            "cluster_auto": True,
   345→            "confidence": "high",
   346→            "detector": detector,
   347→            "file": "",
   348→        }
   349→        result.append(meta_item)
   350→
   351→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   352→    return result
   353→
   354→
   355→def build_work_queue(
   356→    state: StateModel,
   357→    *,
   358→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   359→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   360→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   361→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   362→
   363→    status = resolved_options.status
   364→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   365→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   366→    try:
   367→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   368→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   369→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   370→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   371→
   372→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   373→        state,
   374→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   375→        status_filter=status,
   376→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   377→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   378→    )
   379→
   380→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   381→    if (
   382→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   383→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   384→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   385→    ):
   386→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   387→            state,
   388→            state.get("findings", {}),
   389→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   390→        )
   391→        for item in subjective_items:
   392→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   393→                all_items.append(item)
   394→
   395→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   396→    if resolved_options.plan:
   397→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   398→            all_items=all_items,
   399→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   400→            state=state,
   401→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   402→        )
   403→
   404→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   405→
   406→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   407→    if resolved_options.plan:
   408→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   409→            all_items,
   410→            resolved_options.plan,
   411→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   412→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   413→        )
   414→
   415→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   416→    should_collapse = (
   417→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   418→        and resolved_options.plan
   419→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   420→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   421→    )
   422→    if should_collapse:
   423→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   424→
   425→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   426→
   427→    requested_tier = (
   428→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   429→    )
   430→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   431→    fallback_reason = None
   432→    filtered = all_items
   433→
   434→    if requested_tier is not None:
   435→        filtered = [
   436→            item
   437→            for item in all_items
   438→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   439→        ]
   440→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   441→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   442→            if chosen is not None:
   443→                selected_tier = chosen
   444→                filtered = [
   445→                    item
   446→                    for item in all_items
   447→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   448→                ]
   449→                fallback_reason = (
   450→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   451→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   452→                )
   453→        elif not filtered:
   454→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   455→
   456→    total = len(filtered)
   457→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   458→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   459→
   460→    if resolved_options.explain:
   461→        for item in filtered:
   462→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   463→
   464→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   465→    return {
   466→        "items": filtered,
   467→        "total": total,
   468→        "tier_counts": counts,
   469→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   470→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   471→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   472→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   473→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   474→    }
   475→
   476→
   477→__all__ = [
   478→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   479→    "QueueBuildOptions",

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    79→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    80→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    83→
    84→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    85→    if focus:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    89→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    90→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    91→        if plan_skipped_count:
    92→            print(colorize(
    93→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    94→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    95→                "dim",
    96→            ))
    97→
    98→    if not items:
    99→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    # Determine which items to show
   103→    display_items = items
   104→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   105→        display_items = items[:top]
   106→
   107→    # Build rows
   108→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   109→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   110→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   111→        pos = str(idx)
   112→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   113→
   114→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   115→            tier_str = "T1"
   116→            detector = "synthesis"
   117→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   119→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   120→            cluster_name = ""
   121→        elif kind == "epic":
   122→            tier_str = "T1"
   123→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   124→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   125→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   127→            detector = "epic"
   128→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   129→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   130→        elif kind == "cluster":
   131→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   132→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   133→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   134→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   135→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   136→        else:
   137→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   138→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   139→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   140→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   141→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   142→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   143→
   144→        suffix = ""
   145→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   146→            suffix = " [skip]"
   147→
   148→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   149→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   150→
   151→    print()
   152→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 44, 24]
   153→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   154→
   155→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   156→        remaining = len(items) - top
   157→        print(colorize(
   158→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   159→        ))
   160→    print()
   161→
   162→
   163→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]

    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    34→

    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)

    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()

    81→

   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))

   112→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   113→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   114→

   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:

   327→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:

   334→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"

   336→
   337→        policy = explanation.get("policy")

   341→
   342→
   343→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   344→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   345→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   346→        return
   347→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   348→    if not explain:
   349→        return
   350→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   351→    if available:
   352→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   353→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   354→
   355→
   356→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   357→    if queue.get("items"):
   358→        return False
   359→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   360→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   361→    if tier is not None:
   362→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   363→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   364→        if available:
   365→            commands = " | ".join(
   366→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   367→            )
   368→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   369→    return True
   370→
   371→
   372→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   373→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   374→    if not plan:
   375→        return
   376→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   377→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   378→    if not summary:
   379→        return
   380→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   381→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   382→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   383→
   384→
   385→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   386→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   387→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   388→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   389→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   390→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   391→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   392→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   393→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   394→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   395→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   396→
   397→    print(colorize("  (Meta-plan update needed, T1)", "bold"))
   398→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   399→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   400→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   401→    print()
   402→    if new:
   403→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   404→    if resolved:
   405→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   406→    if epic_count:
   407→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   408→    print()
   409→    print("  Action:   desloppify plan synthesize")
   410→    print("  This will show findings and guide you through creating clusters,")
   411→    print("  setting priorities, and organizing the meta-plan.")
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   414→    print()
   415→    print("  View plan:")
   416→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   417→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   418→
   419→
   420→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   421→    """Render an epic card."""
   422→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   423→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   424→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   425→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   426→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   427→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   428→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   429→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   430→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   431→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   432→
   433→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   434→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   435→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   436→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   437→    if root_cause:
   438→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   439→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   440→    if total_epics:
   441→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   442→    print()
   443→    if action_steps:
   444→        print("  Steps:")
   445→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   446→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   447→        print()
   448→    if finding_ids:
   449→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   450→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   451→        for fid in shown:
   452→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   453→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   454→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   455→        print()
   456→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   457→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   458→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   459→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   460→
   461→
   462→def render_terminal_items(
   463→    items: list[dict],
   464→    dim_scores: dict,
   465→    findings_scoped: dict,
   466→    *,
   467→    group: str,
   468→    explain: bool,
   469→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   470→    plan: dict | None = None,
   471→) -> None:
   472→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   473→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   474→
   475→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   476→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   477→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   478→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   479→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   480→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   481→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   482→
   483→        # Nudge about pending synthesis hidden by focus
   484→        from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   485→        if SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   486→            if not any(it.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed" for it in items):
   487→                print(colorize(
   488→                    "  Note: meta-plan synthesis is pending -- run "
   489→                    "`desloppify plan synthesize` when ready.",
   490→                    "yellow",
   491→                ))
   492→
   493→    if group != "item":
   494→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   495→        return
   496→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   497→        if idx > 0:
   498→            print()
   499→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   500→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   501→
   502→        # Special rendering for synthesis and epic items
   503→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   504→            _render_synthesis_item(item)
   505→            continue
   506→        if kind == "epic":
   507→            _render_epic_item(item)
   508→            continue
   509→
   510→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   511→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   512→        elif len(items) > 1:
   513→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   514→        else:
   515→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   516→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   517→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   518→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   519→
   520→
   521→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   522→    if len(items) != 1:
   523→        return
   524→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   525→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   526→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   527→    if kind != "finding":
   528→        return
   529→    item = items[0]
   530→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   531→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   532→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   533→        primary = item.get(
   534→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   535→        )
   536→        print(f"    {primary}")
   537→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   538→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   539→        return
   540→
   541→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   542→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   543→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   544→        print(f"    {primary}")
   545→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   546→    else:
   547→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   548→
   549→    print(
   550→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   551→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   552→    )
   553→    print(
   554→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   555→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   556→    )
   557→
   558→
   559→def render_followup_nudges(
   560→    state: dict,
   561→    dim_scores: dict,
   562→    findings_scoped: dict,
   563→    *,
   564→    strict_score: float | None,
   565→    target_strict_score: float,
   566→) -> None:
   567→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   568→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   569→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   570→        state,
   571→        subjective_entries,
   572→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   573→        max_quality_items=3,
   574→        max_integrity_items=5,
   575→    )
   576→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   577→    if strict_score is not None:
   578→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   579→        if gap > 0:
   580→            print(
   581→                colorize(
   582→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   583→                    "cyan",
   584→                )
   585→            )
   586→        else:
   587→            print(
   588→                colorize(
   589→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   590→                    "green",
   591→                )
   592→            )
   593→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   594→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   595→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   596→
   597→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   598→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   599→        findings_scoped
   600→    )
   601→    parts: list[str] = []
   602→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   603→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   604→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   605→    open_review = [
   606→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   607→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   608→    ]
   609→    if low_dims:
   610→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   611→    if stale_count:
   612→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   613→    if unassessed_count:
   614→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   615→    if len(open_review):
   616→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   617→    if coverage_open > 0:
   618→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   619→
   620→    if parts:
   621→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   622→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   623→
   624→
   625→__all__ = [
   626→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   627→    "render_followup_nudges",
   628→    "render_queue_header",
   629→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   630→    "render_terminal_items",
   631→    "scorecard_subjective",
   632→    "show_empty_queue",
   633→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   634→]
   635→

1→"""Plan cluster subcommand handlers."""

    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

    22→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    24→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    25→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    26→    plan = load_plan()
    27→    try:
    28→        create_cluster(plan, name, description, action=action)
    29→    except ValueError as ex:
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    31→        return
    32→    save_plan(plan)
    33→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    34→
    35→
    36→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    37→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    38→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    39→        return
    40→
    41→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    42→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    46→    if not finding_ids:
    47→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    48→        return
    49→    try:
    50→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    51→    except ValueError as ex:
    52→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    53→        return
    54→    save_plan(plan)
    55→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    56→
    57→
    58→def _cmd_cluster_remove(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    60→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    61→        return
    62→
    63→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    64→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    65→
    66→    plan = load_plan()
    67→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    68→    if not finding_ids:
    69→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    70→        return
    71→    try:
    72→        count = remove_from_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    73→    except ValueError as ex:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    75→        return
    76→    save_plan(plan)
    77→    print(colorize(f"  Removed {count} item(s) from cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
    78→

    80→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    81→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    82→    plan = load_plan()
    83→    try:

    92→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    95→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)

    97→    plan = load_plan()

    99→    offset: int | None = None

   117→def _print_cluster_member(
   118→    idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None, override: dict | None = None,
   119→) -> None:
   120→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   121→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   122→    if not finding:
   123→        return
   124→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   125→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   126→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   127→    if file:
   128→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   129→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   130→    if summary:
   131→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   132→    if override:
   133→        if override.get("description"):
   134→            print(colorize(f"       Description: {override['description']}", "cyan"))
   135→        if override.get("note"):
   136→            print(colorize(f"       Note: {override['note']}", "dim"))
   137→

   139→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   140→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   141→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   142→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})
   143→
   144→
   145→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   146→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   147→    plan = load_plan()
   148→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   149→    if cluster is None:
   150→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   151→        return
   152→
   153→    # Header
   154→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   155→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   156→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   157→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   158→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   159→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   160→    if desc:
   161→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   162→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   163→    if action:
   164→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   165→
   166→    # Members
   167→    finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   168→    if not finding_ids:
   169→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   170→    else:
   171→        findings = _load_findings_best_effort(args)
   172→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   173→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   174→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
   175→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, findings.get(fid), overrides.get(fid))
   176→
   177→    # Commands
   178→    print()
   179→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   180→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   181→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   182→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   183→
   184→
   185→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   186→    plan = load_plan()
   187→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   188→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   189→    if not clusters:
   190→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   191→        return
   192→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   193→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   194→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   195→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   196→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   197→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   198→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   199→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   200→
   201→
   202→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   203→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   204→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   205→    dispatch = {
   206→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   207→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   208→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   209→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   210→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   211→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   212→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   213→    }
   214→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   215→    if handler is None:
   216→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   217→        return
   218→    handler(args)
   219→
   220→
   221→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]

    22→def cmd_plan_describe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    23→    """Set augmented description on findings."""

    25→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

    32→

    36→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    for fid in finding_ids:
    40→        describe_finding(plan, fid, text or None)
    41→    save_plan(plan)
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Set description on {len(finding_ids)} finding(s).", "green"))

    97→    else:

    99→

   139→
   140→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   141→    count = skip_items(

   154→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   155→    if review_after:

   157→
   158→    # Nudge about synthesis staleness for review findings

   173→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   174→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   175→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   176→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

   184→    if not finding_ids:

   186→        return
   187→
   188→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   189→    save_plan(plan)
   190→
   191→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   192→    if need_reopen:
   193→        from desloppify import state as state_mod
   194→        from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   195→
   196→        state_file = state_path(args)
   197→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   198→        reopened: list[str] = []
   199→        for fid in need_reopen:
   200→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   201→        if reopened:
   202→            state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   203→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   204→
   205→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   206→

   212→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   213→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   214→    from desloppify import state as state_mod
   215→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
   216→
   217→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   218→
   219→    state_file = state_path(args)
   220→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   221→
   222→    reopened: list[str] = []
   223→    for pattern in patterns:
   224→        reopened.extend(
   225→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   226→        )
   227→
   228→    if not reopened:
   229→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   230→        return
   231→
   232→    state_mod.save_state(state_data, state_file)
   233→
   234→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   235→    plan = load_plan()
   236→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   237→    count = 0
   238→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   239→    for fid in reopened:
   240→        if fid in skipped:
   241→            skipped.pop(fid)
   242→            count += 1
   243→        if fid not in order:
   244→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   245→            order.add(fid)
   246→            count += 1
   247→    if count:
   248→        save_plan(plan)
   249→
   250→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   251→    if count:
   252→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   253→
