```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   101→    # detector::security → auto/security
   102→    parts = key.split("::")
   103→    if len(parts) == 2:
   104→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   105→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   106→        if prefix_type == "review":
   107→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   108→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   109→    if len(parts) == 3:
   110→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   111→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   112→
   113→
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→# Description and action generation
   116→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   117→
   118→def _generate_description(
   119→    cluster_name: str,
   120→    members: list[dict],
   121→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   122→    subtype: str | None,
   123→) -> str:
   124→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   125→    count = len(members)
   126→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   127→
   128→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   129→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   130→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   131→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   132→
   133→    if detector == "structural":
   134→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   135→        if len(files) == 1:
   136→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   137→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   138→
   139→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   140→    if subtype:
   141→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   142→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   143→
   144→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   145→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   146→
   147→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   148→
   149→
   150→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   151→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   152→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   153→        return None
   154→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   155→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   156→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   157→        return fixer_name
   158→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   159→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   160→        if subtype in fixer:
   161→            return fixer
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   166→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   167→
   168→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   169→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   170→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   171→    """
   172→    if " — " in guidance:
   173→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   174→    return guidance
   175→
   176→
   177→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   178→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   179→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   180→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   181→}
   182→
   183→
   184→def _generate_action(
   185→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   186→    subtype: str | None,
   187→) -> str:
   188→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   189→
   190→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   191→    """
   192→    if meta is None:
   193→        return "review and fix each finding"
   194→
   195→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   196→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   197→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   198→        if matched_fixer:
   199→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   200→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   201→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   202→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   203→
   204→    if meta.tool == "move":
   205→        return "desloppify move"
   206→
   207→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   208→    if meta.guidance:
   209→        if subtype:
   210→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   211→        return meta.guidance
   212→
   213→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   214→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   215→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   216→    )
   217→
   218→
   219→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   220→# Main algorithm
   221→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   222→
   223→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   224→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   225→
   226→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   227→    """
   228→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   229→    changes = 0
   230→
   231→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   232→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   233→
   234→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   235→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   236→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   237→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   238→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   239→
   240→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   241→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   242→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   243→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   244→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   245→            continue
   246→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   247→            continue
   248→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   252→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   253→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   254→        if key is None:
   255→            continue
   256→
   257→        groups[key].append(fid)
   258→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   259→
   260→    # Drop singleton groups
   261→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   262→
   263→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   264→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   265→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   266→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   267→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   268→            if ck:
   269→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   270→
   271→    now = utc_now()
   272→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   273→
   274→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   275→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   276→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   277→
   278→        # Representative finding for metadata
   279→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   280→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   281→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   282→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   283→
   284→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   285→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   286→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   287→
   288→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   289→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   290→
   291→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   292→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   293→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   294→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   295→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   296→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   297→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   298→                if new_ids:
   299→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   300→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   301→                    changes += 1
   302→            else:
   303→                # Replace membership wholesale
   304→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   305→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   306→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   307→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   308→                    cluster["description"] = description
   309→                    cluster["action"] = action
   310→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   311→                    changes += 1
   312→        else:
   313→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   314→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   315→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   316→                # Append a disambiguator
   317→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   318→
   319→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   320→                "name": cluster_name,
   321→                "description": description,
   322→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   323→                "created_at": now,
   324→                "updated_at": now,
   325→                "auto": True,
   326→                "cluster_key": key,
   327→                "action": action,
   328→                "user_modified": False,
   329→            }
   330→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   331→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   332→            changes += 1
   333→
   334→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   335→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   336→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   337→        for fid in member_ids:
   338→            if fid not in overrides:
   339→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   340→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   341→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   342→
   343→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   344→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   345→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   346→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   347→    )
   348→    unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   349→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   350→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   351→    )
   352→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   353→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   354→    )
   355→
   356→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   357→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   358→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   359→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   360→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   361→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   362→
   363→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   364→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   365→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   366→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   367→            new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
   368→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   369→                cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
   370→                cluster["description"] = description
   371→                cluster["action"] = action
   372→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   373→                changes += 1
   374→        else:
   375→            clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
   376→                "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
   377→                "description": description,
   378→                "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
   379→                "created_at": now,
   380→                "updated_at": now,
   381→                "auto": True,
   382→                "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
   383→                "action": action,
   384→                "user_modified": False,
   385→            }
   386→            existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
   387→            changes += 1
   388→
   389→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   390→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
   391→        for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
   392→            if fid not in overrides:
   393→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   394→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   395→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   396→
   397→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   398→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   399→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   400→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   401→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   402→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   403→
   404→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   405→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   406→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   407→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   408→            new_ids = set(stale_queue_ids)
   409→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   410→                cluster["finding_ids"] = stale_queue_ids
   411→                cluster["description"] = description
   412→                cluster["action"] = action
   413→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   414→                changes += 1
   415→        else:
   416→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   417→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   418→                "description": description,
   419→                "finding_ids": stale_queue_ids,
   420→                "created_at": now,
   421→                "updated_at": now,
   422→                "auto": True,
   423→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   424→                "action": action,
   425→                "user_modified": False,
   426→            }
   427→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   428→            changes += 1
   429→
   430→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   431→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   432→        for fid in stale_queue_ids:
   433→            if fid not in overrides:
   434→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   435→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   436→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   437→
   438→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   439→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   440→        cluster = clusters[name]
   441→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   442→            continue
   443→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   444→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   445→            continue
   446→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   447→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   448→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   449→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   450→            if alive:
   451→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   452→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   453→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   454→                    changes += 1
   455→                continue
   456→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   457→        # Delete stale cluster
   458→        del clusters[name]
   459→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   460→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   461→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   462→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   463→                override["cluster"] = None
   464→                override["updated_at"] = now
   465→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   466→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   467→        changes += 1
   468→
   469→    plan["updated"] = now
   470→    return changes
   471→
   472→
   473→__all__ = [
   474→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   475→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   476→]
   477→
```

```
1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
     8→
     9→
    10→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    11→    state: StateModel,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    15→    status_filter: str = "open",
    16→) -> list[str]:
    17→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    18→
    19→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    20→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    21→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    22→    """
    23→    seen: set[str] = set()
    24→    result: list[str] = []
    25→
    26→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    27→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    28→    if plan is not None:
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    30→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    31→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    32→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    33→
    34→    for pattern in patterns:
    35→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    36→        if matches:
    37→            for finding in matches:
    38→                fid = finding["id"]
    39→                if fid not in seen:
    40→                    seen.add(fid)
    41→                    result.append(fid)
    42→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    43→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    44→            seen.add(pattern)
    45→            result.append(pattern)
    46→    return result
    47→
    48→
    49→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    50→
```

```
1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→from fnmatch import fnmatch
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens
     9→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    11→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":
   165→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   166→            return "desloppify review --prepare"
   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"
   168→    return f'desloppify plan done "{item.get("id", "")}" --note "<what you did>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'

   171→def subjective_strict_scores(state: dict) -> dict[str, float]:
   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   173→    if not dim_scores:

   175→
   176→    entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   177→    scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   178→    for entry in entries:

   180→        if not name:

   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)

   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))

   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   297→    """Build a synthetic T1 queue item for pending epic synthesis.
   298→
   299→    Returns None if synthesis is not needed (no synthesis::pending in queue).
   300→    """
   301→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   302→        SYNTHESIS_ID,
   303→        review_finding_snapshot_hash,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   307→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
   308→        return None
   309→
   310→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   311→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   312→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   313→
   314→    # Count review findings
   315→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   316→    review_ids = [
   317→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   318→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   319→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   320→    ]
   321→    total = len(review_ids)
   322→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   323→    new_since_last = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid not in synthesized)
   324→    resolved_since_last = sum(1 for fid in synthesized if fid not in {r for r in review_ids})
   325→
   326→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   327→    pending_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status", "pending") == "pending")
   328→    in_progress_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "in_progress")
   329→    completed_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "completed")
   330→
   331→    trigger = "initial synthesis needed"
   332→    if last_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   333→        if new_since_last > resolved_since_last:
   334→            trigger = "new findings imported"
   335→        elif resolved_since_last > 0:
   336→            trigger = "findings resolved"
   337→        else:
   338→            trigger = "findings changed"
   339→
   340→    return {
   341→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   342→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   343→        "tier": 1,
   344→        "effective_tier": 1,
   345→        "confidence": "high",
   346→        "detector": "synthesis",
   347→        "file": ".",
   348→        "summary": f"Meta-plan update: {total} review findings need integration into epics",
   349→        "status": "open",
   350→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   351→        "detail": {
   352→            "total_review_findings": total,
   353→            "new_since_last": new_since_last,
   354→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since_last,
   355→            "trigger": trigger,
   356→            "epic_count": len(epics),
   357→            "pending_epics": pending_epics,
   358→            "in_progress_epics": in_progress_epics,
   359→            "completed_epics": completed_epics,
   360→        },
   361→    }
   362→
   363→
   364→__all__ = [
   365→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   366→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   367→    "build_subjective_items",
   368→    "build_synthesis_item",
   369→    "detail_dict",
   370→    "is_review_finding",
   371→    "is_subjective_finding",
   372→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   373→    "review_finding_weight",
   374→    "scope_matches",
   375→    "slugify",
   376→    "status_matches",
   377→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   378→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   379→]
   380→
```

```
1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    88→
    89→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before everything else (T0-equivalent).
    90→    if kind == "synthesis_needed":
    91→        return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    92→    if kind == "epic":
    93→        dep_order = 999
    94→        detail = item.get("detail")
    95→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    96→            dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
    97→        return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    98→
    99→    if kind == "cluster":
   100→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   101→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
   102→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
   103→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
   104→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   105→        )
   106→        return (
   107→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
   108→            action_pri,
   109→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   110→            item.get("id", ""),
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   114→        return (
   115→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   116→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   117→            subjective_score_value(item),
   118→            item.get("id", ""),
   119→        )
   120→
   121→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   122→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   123→    return (
   124→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   125→        0,
   126→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   127→        -review_weight,
   128→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   129→        item.get("id", ""),
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   134→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   135→
   136→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   137→        return {
   138→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   139→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   140→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   141→            "policy": (
   142→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   143→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   144→            ),
   145→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   146→        }
   147→
   148→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   149→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   150→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   151→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   152→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   153→    if is_subjective:
   154→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   155→    elif is_review:
   156→        ranking_factors = [
   157→            "tier asc",
   158→            "confidence asc",
   159→            "review_weight desc",
   160→            "count desc",
   161→            "id asc",
   162→        ]
   163→    else:
   164→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   165→    explain = {
   166→        "kind": "finding",
   167→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   168→        "confidence": confidence,
   169→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   170→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   171→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   172→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   173→    }
   174→    if is_review:
   175→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   176→    if is_subjective:
   177→        explain["policy"] = (
   178→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   179→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   180→        )
   181→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   182→    return explain
   183→
   184→
   185→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   186→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   187→    for item in items:
   188→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   189→            # Count each member at its own tier
   190→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   191→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   192→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   193→            continue
   194→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   195→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   196→    return counts
   197→
   198→
   199→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   200→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   201→    if not available:
   202→        return None
   203→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   204→
   205→
   206→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   207→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   208→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   209→    for item in items:
   210→        if group == "file":
   211→            key = item.get("file", "")
   212→        elif group == "detector":
   213→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   214→        elif group == "tier":
   215→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   216→        elif group == "cluster":
   217→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   218→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   219→        else:
   220→            key = "items"
   221→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   222→    return grouped
   223→
   224→
   225→__all__ = [
   226→    "build_finding_items",
   227→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   228→    "item_explain",
   229→    "item_sort_key",
   230→    "tier_counts",
   231→    "subjective_score_value",
   232→    "group_queue_items",
   233→]
   234→
```

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→    # Include epic finding_ids and dismissed lists
   147→    for epic in plan.get("epics", {}).values():
   148→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   149→        referenced_ids.update(epic.get("dismissed", []))
   150→
   151→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   152→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   153→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   154→    referenced_ids.discard(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   155→    referenced_ids = {
   156→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   157→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   158→    }
   159→
   160→    # Check each referenced ID
   161→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   162→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   163→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   164→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   165→                result.changes += 1
   166→
   167→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   168→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   169→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   170→
   171→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   172→    if resurfaced:
   173→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   174→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   175→
   176→    # Prune old superseded entries
   177→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   178→    result.pruned = pruned
   179→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   180→
   181→    # Clean up epics: remove dead findings, mark completed, delete empty
   182→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   183→    dead_ids = {fid for fid in result.superseded}
   184→    for epic_name in list(epics.keys()):
   185→        epic = epics[epic_name]
   186→        fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   187→        alive = [fid for fid in fids if fid not in dead_ids]
   188→        if len(alive) != len(fids):
   189→            epic["finding_ids"] = alive
   190→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   191→            result.changes += 1
   192→        # Mark completed if all findings resolved
   193→        if not alive and epic.get("status") != "completed":
   194→            epic["status"] = "completed"
   195→            epic["updated_at"] = now
   196→        # Delete empty epics
   197→        if not alive:
   198→            del epics[epic_name]
   199→
   200→    return result
   201→
   202→
   203→__all__ = [
   204→    "ReconcileResult",
   205→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   206→]
   207→
```

```
   172→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   173→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   174→    return (
   175→        finding_id == pattern
   176→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   177→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   178→        or (
   179→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   180→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   181→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   182→        )
   183→    )
   184→
```

## Detailed Implementation Plan

Below is the implementation guidance for all 9 items, in the prescribed priority order. Each item includes exact file paths, line numbers, code locations, and the precise nature of the changes needed.

---

### Item 1: Bug 2 -- Skip -> unskip loses queue position

**Problem:** When a finding is skipped, its position in `queue_order` is discarded. When unskipped, it is appended to the end via `plan["queue_order"].append(fid)` at line 181 of `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`.

**Files to modify:**

**A. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`**

Add an `original_position: int | None` field to `SkipEntry` (line 21-29). This TypedDict uses `total=False`, so adding the field is backward-compatible with existing plan data (missing key defaults to `None` on `.get()`).

```python
class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    finding_id: Required[str]
    kind: Required[str]
    reason: str | None
    note: str | None
    attestation: str | None
    created_at: str
    review_after: int | None
    skipped_at_scan: int
    original_position: int | None  # NEW: queue position before skip
```

**B. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`**

In `skip_items()` (line 130-159): Before calling `_remove_id_from_lists()`, record the finding's current position in `queue_order`:

```python
def skip_items(plan, finding_ids, *, kind="temporary", ...):
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    now = utc_now()
    count = 0
    skipped = plan["skipped"]
    order = plan["queue_order"]
    for fid in finding_ids:
        # Record original position before removal
        original_pos = order.index(fid) if fid in order else None
        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
        skipped[fid] = {
            "finding_id": fid,
            "kind": kind,
            "reason": reason,
            "note": note,
            "attestation": attestation,
            "created_at": now,
            "review_after": review_after,
            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
            "original_position": original_pos,  # NEW
        }
        count += 1
    return count
```

In `unskip_items()` (line 162-183): Instead of appending to the end, restore to the original position (or nearest valid position if the queue has shrunk):

```python
def unskip_items(plan, finding_ids):
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    count = 0
    need_reopen = []
    skipped = plan["skipped"]
    for fid in finding_ids:
        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
        if entry is not None:
            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
                need_reopen.append(fid)
            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
                original_pos = entry.get("original_position")
                if original_pos is not None:
                    # Clamp to current queue length
                    insert_at = min(original_pos, len(plan["queue_order"]))
                    plan["queue_order"].insert(insert_at, fid)
                else:
                    plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
            count += 1
    return count, need_reopen
```

Also update `resurface_stale_skips()` (line 186-209) to use the same position-restoration logic (it currently also appends to end on line 207).

**Tests:** Add tests in a new or existing test file verifying: (a) skip records position, (b) unskip restores to that position, (c) unskip clamps if queue shrunk, (d) unskip of entry with `None` position appends to end.

---

### Item 2: Add finding IDs to queue table (Item 4 from priority list)

**Problem:** The queue table shows no identifiers users can reference in subsequent commands.

**File to modify: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`**

At lines 136-148, modify the summary construction to append the 8-char hash suffix. For individual findings (the `else` branch at line 136), the finding ID is available via `item.get("id", "")`. The ID format is `detector::file::hash` -- extract the last `::` segment.

Change the summary line construction (around line 148):

```python
# After the existing summary computation, before constructing summary_display:
if kind == "finding" or kind not in ("synthesis_needed", "epic", "cluster"):
    fid = item.get("id", "")
    # Extract last segment (hash suffix) from finding ID
    suffix_parts = fid.rsplit("::", 1)
    hash_suffix = suffix_parts[-1][:8] if len(suffix_parts) > 1 else fid[:8]
    if hash_suffix:
        summary = f"{summary} [{hash_suffix}]"

suffix = ""
if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    suffix = " [skip]"

summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
```

Note: The `_truncate` function truncates at 48 chars. With the `[abc12345]` suffix adding ~11 chars, consider increasing the summary width from 48 to 56 or widening column 4 from 44 to 52 (line 152). Alternatively, include the hash in the truncation budget by appending it before truncation. The approach above appends before truncation so the hash is part of the budget, which is the simpler path.

For cluster and epic items, the ID is the cluster/epic name which is already visible in the Cluster column, so no hash is needed.

### Item 4: Bug 1 -- cluster delete does not stick for auto-clusters

**Problem:** `auto_cluster_findings()` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` recreates auto-clusters on each scan even after the user deletes them.

**Files to modify:**

**A. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`**

Add `deleted_clusters: list[str]` to `PlanModel` (line 94-106) and its default in `empty_plan()` (line 109-125):

```python
    # ... existing fields ...
    deleted_clusters: list[str]  # NEW: cluster names explicitly deleted by user
```

```python
    plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
```

**B. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py`**

In `delete_cluster()` (line 337-355), when deleting an auto-cluster, record it in `deleted_clusters`:

```python
def delete_cluster(plan, name):
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
    if cluster is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")

    # If auto-cluster, record deletion to prevent regeneration
    if cluster.get("auto"):
        deleted_list = plan.setdefault("deleted_clusters", [])
        cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
        if cluster_key and cluster_key not in deleted_list:
            deleted_list.append(cluster_key)

    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    # ... rest unchanged ...
```

Note: We store the `cluster_key` (e.g. `"auto::unused"`) rather than the cluster name, because the cluster name is derived from the key and could theoretically change if disambiguators are added.

**C. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`**

### Item 6: Focus silent shift warning (Item 6)

**Problem:** When skip/done empties the focused cluster, `render_terminal_items()` silently shows an empty or different view with no explanation.

In `render_terminal_items()` at lines 476-491, after the focus header block (which ends at line 491), add a check for empty focus:

```python
    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))

        # NEW: Warn if focused cluster is empty in current view
        if len(items) == 0:
            print(colorize(
                f"\n  Warning: Focus cluster '{cluster_name}' has no remaining items.",
                "yellow",
            ))
            print(colorize(

                "dim",
            ))

        # existing synthesis nudge code...
```

This handles the case where items is already filtered by cluster. The caller (`cmd_next` / the next command) uses the focus to filter items, so when the focused cluster is empty `items` will be `[]`.

### Item 7: Better "no matching findings" error (Item 8)

**Problem:** Seven call sites print `"No matching findings found."` with no guidance on pattern formats.

**Approach:** Create a shared helper function in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/_resolve.py` (it already owns pattern resolution) and call it from all sites.

**File to modify: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/_resolve.py`**

Add a helper function:

```python
from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize

def print_no_match_hint(patterns: list[str]) -> None:
    """Print 'no matching findings' with guidance on pattern formats."""
    pat_str = " ".join(patterns) if patterns else "(none)"
    print(colorize(f"  No matching findings for: {pat_str}", "yellow"))
    print(colorize("  Pattern formats:", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    finding-id       — exact finding ID (use `plan queue` to see IDs)", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    detector::       — all findings from a detector (prefix match)", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    detector         — all findings from that detector", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    path/to/file     — all findings in that file", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    abc12345         — 8+ hex chars match hash suffix of finding ID", "dim"))
    print(colorize("    glob*pattern     — glob match on finding ID", "dim"))
```

Add `print_no_match_hint` to `__all__`.

Then update all 7 call sites to import and use `print_no_match_hint(patterns)` instead of the generic message:

1. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/move_handlers.py` line 27
2. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/override_handlers.py` lines 36, 59, 119, 185
3. `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cluster_handlers.py` lines 47, 69

Each site already has access to `patterns` as a local variable.

### Item 8: Bug 3 -- `--include-skipped` shows nothing

**Problem:** In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`, `_apply_plan_order()` only includes skipped items if they appear in the `items` list passed in. But `build_finding_items()` already filters out non-open findings, so skipped items (which are often `wontfix`/`false_positive` in state) never appear.

**File to modify: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`**

In `_apply_plan_order()` (line 59-145), when `include_skipped=True`, build synthetic display items for plan-skipped entries that have no corresponding item in `by_id`. This requires access to `state["findings"]` which is not currently passed to this function.

**Option A (preferred):** Pass `state` as an optional parameter to `_apply_plan_order` when `include_skipped=True`.

Modify the function signature:

```python
def _apply_plan_order(
    items: list[dict],
    plan: dict,
    *,
    include_skipped: bool = False,
    cluster: str | None = None,
    state: dict | None = None,  # NEW: needed for synthetic skipped items
) -> list[dict]:
```

After building `by_id` (line 81-83), inject synthetic items for skipped IDs not already in `by_id`:

```python
    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries not in the work queue
    if include_skipped and state:
        findings = state.get("findings", {})
        for skip_id, skip_entry in skipped_map.items():
            if skip_id in by_id:
                continue  # Already in queue
            finding = findings.get(skip_id)
            if finding:
                synthetic = {
                    "id": skip_id,
                    "kind": "finding",
                    "detector": finding.get("detector", ""),
                    "file": finding.get("file", ""),
                    "summary": finding.get("summary", ""),
                    "tier": finding.get("tier", 3),
                    "effective_tier": finding.get("tier", 3),
                    "confidence": finding.get("confidence", "medium"),
                    "status": finding.get("status", "open"),
                }
                by_id[skip_id] = synthetic
            else:
                # Finding no longer in state -- create minimal placeholder
                by_id[skip_id] = {
                    "id": skip_id,
                    "kind": "finding",
                    "detector": "unknown",
                    "file": "",
                    "summary": f"(skipped — no longer in scan: {skip_id})",
                    "tier": 3,
                    "effective_tier": 3,
                    "confidence": "low",
                    "status": "skipped",
                }
```

Then update the call site in `build_work_queue()` (line 408) to pass `state`:

```python
    if resolved_options.plan:
        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
            all_items,
            resolved_options.plan,
            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,  # NEW
        )
```

Note: `build_work_queue` receives `state` as its first positional argument (line 356), so it is readily available.
